## 10.1 Performance pass

- **Date:** 2026-10-25
- **Branch:** feat/step-10.1-performance
- **Summary:** SPEC §14, §8.3, §15.2; `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`. `scripts/perf.sh` now measures every target of §14: typing, scrolling, the 10 MB and 200 MB opens, the cold start in three cache states and with four restored tabs, Emetos's own share of the start (before the window and the first build), idle with and without focus, memory with its split by mapping, and Go benchmarks for the UI thread, fuzzy search, quick open and grammar loading (`--no-go`, `--no-huge`, `--profile dir`, `PERF_KEEP`); two bugs of the old script fixed (typing and scrolling counted the first frame; the first-frame wait could be seconds late). Dev profiling in `emetos_dev` builds: `EMETOS_PROFILE_DIR` with SIGUSR1 (heap, allocs, goroutines, memstats) and SIGUSR2 (CPU on/off), `EMETOS_PROFILE_START=cpu`. Fixes: (1) **a tab character made Glutter load all 62 monospace system fonts** (no font has a glyph for U+0009): a 288-byte bundled font with an empty tab glyph (`fonts.ControlsFont`) goes first in the families of rows with a tab; resident memory 738 → 418 MB, the first build of a 10 MB file 206 → 12 ms, a restore with four tabs 725 → 406 MB; (2) **every tokenizer slice made the next frame re-pair every bracket of the file**: `brackets.Index.Invalidate` notes the dirty lines and the next question re-reads only those on the UI thread, re-pairing only when brackets changed; scrolling build 8.3 → 1.5 ms average, worst 25.7 → 5.6 ms; (3) **a closed Output panel drew a frame on every log line**: `output.ViewOpts.Visible`; idle frames 31 → 21 per 10 s; (4) **grammar rules built in the first frame**: `Process.WarmGrammars` compiles the grammars of the files the window opens with on a goroutine while the renderer starts, and `Tokenizer.Ensure` no longer waits for a grammar another goroutine is compiling; (5) **the synchronous tokenization budget was spent by layout, paint and the minimap each**: one 4 ms budget per frame shared by all three (was 6 ms each); scrolling to unseen highlighted lines 7.8 → 4.7 ms. The targets still missed headless were decided by me (SPEC §14 rows revised).
- **Main files:** `scripts/perf.sh`, `cmd/emetos/{main,profile_dev,profile_off}.go`, `internal/gui/kit/fonts/{fonts,controls}.go`, `internal/gui/editor/{text,tokens}.go`, `internal/core/brackets/index.go`, `internal/core/textmate/grammar.go`, `internal/core/tokens/tokenizer.go`, `internal/gui/app/{panels,process,workspaces}.go`, `internal/gui/views/output/view.go`, `docs/{PERFORMANCE,SPEC,GLUTTER-ISSUES,MANUAL-CHECKS}.md`.
- **Tests:** `TestTheFontOfTheTabChangesNoMeasure`, `TestTextWithATabPutsTheFontOfTheTabFirst`, `controls_test.go`; `TestNewTokensKeepThePairsWhenTheBracketsAreTheSame`, `TestInvalidateReadsNothingFromTheSource`, `TestNotedLinesFollowAnEdit` (checked by mutation); `TestAHiddenPanelAsksForNoFrames`, `TestIdleWorkbenchDrawsOnlyTheBlink` (fails without fix 3); `TestEnsureDoesNotWaitForTheRulesOfTheGrammar`, `TestStartWarmsTheGrammarOfTheFilesOfTheCommandLine`, `TestWarmMakesTheRulesBeforeTheFirstLine`; `TestAFrameTokenizesWithinOneBudget` (fails with 3 slices instead of 1 without fix 5); grammar benchmarks in `core/language/bench_test.go`. Existing tests changed: `TestSources` and `TestFontsAreGoMonoAndTheIconFonts` pin the list of bundled fonts, which gains `emetos-controls`. No golden changed.
- **Results (headless, `scripts/perf.sh -n 5 -s 10`, medians):** typing 2.5 ms average build (benchmark 0.15 ms) — met; scrolling 1.5 ms average, worst 5.6 ms — met; 10 MB open 110–140 ms over the empty window — met (revised row); 200 MB open 1.28 s — met; cold start 920–940 ms with Emetos's share 21–28 ms (76–77 ms with four tabs) — Emetos's share met, the 400 ms with the GPU pending the session run; idle focused 1.0–1.3% at 2 frames a second (llvmpipe) — met by the headless rule, pending the session run; idle unfocused 0 frames — met; memory 418 MB against the counter's 280 MB — met by the revised target (+150 MB); fuzzy search 2.3–9.9 ms on all cores — met. Details in `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** the step asks for none; the numbers are in `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`.
- **Manual checks:** pending, see docs/MANUAL-CHECKS.md (`scripts/perf.sh --session` on the user's desktop with the GPU).
- **Glutter issues:** a new entry: a character no font has makes Glutter load every font of the style (the tab); worked around with the controls font (`// Glutter:` in `textStyle.families`). The Vulkan-first entry stays.
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** the missed targets are decided in `docs/DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW.md` entries 41–44 (SPEC §14 revised). `TestACloseWhoseAutoSaveFailsAsksAndDontSaveIsNotUndoneByTheFocus` failed once in three full race runs under load and passed every rerun; watch it.

## 11.1 The extension contract and its lifetime

- **Date:** 2026-10-25
- **Branch:** feat/step-11.1-extension-contract
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.15, D18, §5.2, §5.3. The public package `extension/` (no Emetos imports): `Factory`, `Extension` (`Manifest`, `Activate(ctx, api)`), `Manifest` (ID, name, version, description, activation events, declared commands, menus and keybindings; `CheckManifest`), the event helpers, `ScopeInfo`, and an `API` bound to one scope: `Scope`, `Log`, `Post`, `Commands` (`Handle` a declared command, `Register`), `Keybindings`, `Menus`, `Settings` (`Register` under `<id>.`, `Get`, `OnChange`, `SettingValue[T]`), `Notifications` (with actions), `StatusBar` (items with `Update`, `SetVisible`, `Dispose`), `Documents` (`Read`, `Open`). `extensions/all.go` (`extensions.All()`, empty) and the test extension `extensions/internal/testext`. The host `internal/gui/exthost`: one activation per workspace tab, by `onStartup`, `onLanguage:`, `onFile:` (at the start and on file index changes), `onCommand:` (declared commands are implicit events), `onView:`/`onWindowTab:` (validated, fired from 11.5/11.7), with events replayed for an extension enabled later; the scope's `ctx` is cancelled and every contribution removed when the tab closes or the extension is disabled; every call into an extension is guarded (factory, `Activate`, command handler, status bar click, notification action, settings listener, posted function: each its own tag), and a failure removes the extension's contributions in that tab only, logs the error with its route, shows one notification with "Show Log" and marks it failed on the page. Commands and menus are per-workspace forks (`Workspace.Menus`; `command.Registry.Unregister`, `MenuRegistry.AddRemovable`/`Fork`, `settings.Registry.Unregister`); extension keys are a layer between the defaults and the user's keybindings. `extensions.disabled` (application scope). The page "Extensions" (`workbench.extensions.action.showInstalledExtensions`, Ctrl+Shift+X, View/Preferences/Manage menus; an editor input restored with the window) lists every manifest with its state (active in which tabs, not active yet, failed with the box text, disabled) and Enable/Disable. Archtest rules `ExtensionImportsInternal`, `ExtensionImportsExtension`, `ExtensionImportsForbidden`, `ExtensionsImportedByInternal` with testdata. CLAUDE.md's format command and layout list the new folders.
- **Main files:** `extension/`, `extensions/`, `internal/gui/exthost/`, `internal/gui/extensionsui/`, `internal/gui/app/{extensions,extensionspane}.go`, `internal/core/editorgroup/extensionsinput.go`, `internal/core/command`, `internal/core/settings`, `internal/archtest/`, `cmd/emetos/main.go`, `docs/SPEC.md` §5.2, §5.3, §10.15.
- **Tests:** `extension` (`CheckManifest` with routes, events, `SettingValue`); `exthost` (probes and invalid manifests, each activation event, two scopes two instances, scope close cancels `ctx` and removes contributions, settings ref-counted across scopes, every containment boundary with route tests, one notification only, the other tab unaffected, `extensions.disabled` with replay, retry after failure, the API after deactivation, the misuse tags, `Post`, shutdown); `app` (a workspace is a scope, per-workspace contributions, keys/menus/status items removed on release, events through the real document-open and file-index paths, a command panic in one workspace only, keys through the real dispatcher, the page with Disable/Enable, the documents adapter); `extensionsui`, `editorgroup`, core `Unregister`/`AddRemovable`/`Fork`; archtest's four new cases and the clean tree. Existing tests changed: the menu tests count the new Extensions rows, `TestEveryWorkbenchCommandHasAHandler` knows the app binds the new command. Goldens: new `extensions_page_{dark,light}`; changed for the Extensions menu row (`manage_menu_themes`, `themes_menu`, `appearance_menu`, `open_recent_menu`, `workbench_view_menu`) and the settings editor's new Extensions group (`settings_top`); viewed.
- **Not ported:** VS Code's extension host process, marketplace and `package.json` contributions (non-goals); `workspaceContains` is `onFile`.

## 11.2 Events

- **Date:** 2026-10-25
- **Branch:** feat/step-11.2-events
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.15 "Events". `api.Events()`: `Publish`, `Subscribe` (patterns: `*` one segment, a final `**` one or more), `Request(ctx, topic, value, reply)` (callback, 10 s default timeout on the window's timers, `EventNoHandler`, `EventTimeout`, `EventCanceled`, the handler's error as `EventHandlerFailed`), `Handle` and `HandleAsync` (reply once from any goroutine); `Manifest.Events` declares published (optionally fast), handled and subscribed topics; an extension publishes and handles only its own id's topics and the ones it declares (`EventTopicNotOwned`), `emetos.*` ids are reserved. Values are JSON copies; delivery is on the UI thread in order per publisher and topic, never re-entrant; fast topics are batched per frame (one event with `Batch` per publisher and topic) and flushed before a later normal event; handlers or subscribers over 50 ms are logged; subscriber, handler and reply-callback panics fail the extension in that scope (own tags). The core events published by every workspace scope: `emetos.workspace.opened`/`closed`, `emetos.windowTab.activated`, `emetos.document.opened`/`changed` (fast, with the changes)/`saved`/`closed`, `emetos.editor.activeChanged` (coalesced per turn), `emetos.editor.selectionChanged` (fast). Delivery and handler lookup go through `Host.targets`/`Host.handlerFor`, where step 11.3 adds the links. `api.Documents().Read` was already built in 11.1.
- **Main files:** `extension/{events,api,extension,manifest}.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/{bus,events,scope,instance,host}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{coreevents,extensions,workspacemodel,group,navigation}.go`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.15.
- **Tests:** `exthost/events_test.go` (delivery and order, subscribe/cancel/deactivate, patterns, ownership and misuse with routes, copies in both directions, request and reply with no handler, timeout on a fake clock with a late reply dropped, `ctx` deadline and cancel, handler error and panic, requester and handler gone, async reply from a goroutine, subscriber and reply-callback panics, the slow log at 50 ms, 100 values one batch, fast never overtaking, re-entrancy, `PublishCore`, workspace opened/closed, no cross-scope delivery, `ExtensionTopicForeign`); `extension_test.go` (`CheckManifest` with topics and reserved ids, topic/pattern tables, `EventValue(s)`); `app/coreevents_test.go` (open, active editor, selection, close, empty group, 100 edits in one frame give one batch of 100, undo, save, Save As with `previousUri`, workspace closed before deactivation, two tabs with no crossing and `windowTab.activated`). Existing tests changed: a test used the now reserved id `emetos`; the Post test counts the `workspace.opened` delivery; the test recorder gained `Events()`.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** the step asks for none.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none

## 11.3 Event sharing between window tabs

- **Date:** 2026-10-25
- **Branch:** feat/step-11.3-event-sharing
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.15 "Event sharing". `$XDG_STATE_HOME/emetos/event-sharing.json` (`storage.SharingStore`: versioned `{"version":1,"links":[{"from","to","events"}],"private":[...]}`, atomic, 0600, read at start, written on each change from the UI). A tab's sharing id is its folder (No Folder tabs have none). Delivery across scopes is checked per event at delivery: only along a saved or session link `from → to` carrying the topic, never out of a private workspace; queued events stop when a link is removed; fast batches cross as one. `Manifest.Events.CrossTab` lists the topics an extension may get from other tabs; `Subscribe(..., FromOtherScopes()|FromScopes(...))`, `Request(..., ToOtherScopes()|ToScopes(...))` (needs the link from the handler's tab to the requester's; the reply is checked again on arrival), `Documents().Read(..., FromScope(id))` (needs a link carrying `emetos.document.changed`). The question "Let events from A reach B?" in the receiving tab (Allow saves the link, This Session Only keeps it in memory, Deny/Escape is remembered until the extension is activated again; one question per pair at a time; topics outside `CrossTab` are never asked about). The page "Extensions › Event Sharing" (`emetos.extensions.manageEventSharing`, an editor input restored with the window, an "Event Sharing..." button on the Extensions page): saved and session links as `from → to` with topics and Remove, every open tab and every private scope with "Private: never send events to other tabs". `emetos.sharing.revoked` (`{from, fromName, topics, reason}`) in the receiving tab when a link is removed or its source turns private. A file that cannot be written keeps the link for the session with a warning; a corrupt file is ignored with a warning.
- **Main files:** `internal/core/storage/sharing.go`, `internal/core/editorgroup/eventsharinginput.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/{sharing,bus,events,api,host,scope}.go`, `internal/gui/extensionsui/{sharingpage,page}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{eventsharingpane,process,extensions,extensionspane,panes}.go`, `extension/`, `internal/core/command/builtin.go`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.15.
- **Tests:** `exthost/sharing_test.go` (`notes → office` delivers and nothing goes back, a topic-limited link, removal stops queued events and tells the receiver, private sends nothing and refuses reads, requests and being asked, the three answers with the file written or not, dedup and re-ask after reactivation, undeclared topics never asked, links survive a restart, unnamed tabs, requests and replies along links with ask/deny/timeout, a reply stopped by a removed link, cross-tab reads, fast batches, write failure falls back to a session link); `storage/sharing_test.go` (direction, merge, private, session links never written, restart round trip with 0600, unusable files and write/encode failures route-tested); `extension` (`PatternWithin`, scope filters, the `CrossTab` check); `app/eventsharing_test.go` (the dialog's four answers, a link allowed before a restart works after it, a corrupt file ignored with a warning, No Folder tabs, the page flow with Private, Remove, the revoked event and the file contents, the editor kind restores); all in isolated folders. Existing tests changed: the command coverage allowlist gains the new command; the exthost rig gives tabs ids and a question seam. Goldens: new `event_sharing_page_{dark,light}`, `event_sharing_question_{dark,light}` (fixed folder `/tmp/emetos-golden-sharing` made and removed by the test, so the shown paths are stable); `extensions_page_{dark,light}` changed by the new button; viewed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** the step asks for none; the page as the golden

  ![11.3 Event Sharing page](../internal/gui/app/testdata/goldens/event_sharing_page_dark.png)

## 11.4 Language packs move to `extensions/`

- **Date:** 2026-10-25
- **Branch:** feat/step-11.4-language-packs
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.15, §8.1, D13. The 22 bundled language packs moved from `internal/core/language/assets/` to `extensions/<pack>/assets/` (VS Code's `package.json`, language configurations, `syntaxes/`, `cgmanifest.json`; `go:embed`), one extension per VS Code extension folder with ids `emetos.<pack>`: golang, json, yaml, markdown, python, javascript, typescript, html, css, scss, less, xml, sql, shellscript, makefile, docker, ini, cpp, rust, diff, git, gomod; each is `langpack.New(Spec{...})` with a no-op `Activate` at `onStartup`. `extension.Contributes.Languages` (an `fs.FS`) is the declarative language contribution; the host registers every pack and its settings defaults when it is made, before any tab (`language.Registry.AddNamedExtension`/`RemoveExtension`/`Version`, numbers kept for a pack that comes back; `settings.Registry.UnregisterLanguageDefaults`), holds a pack per tab and removes it when the last tab lets go; `Host.SyncLanguages` follows `extensions.disabled` before any file is restored or opened; `Process.Grammar` rebuilds its cache when the registry changes. The core keeps plain text only (`language.Bundled` removed). Tests get the same registry through `internal/core/language/languagetest.Bundled()` over `packfs` (the pack folders found from the repository root; test-only, not in the release binary). `scripts/update-assets.sh` writes into the new folders (run: byte-identical); NOTICE notes the new location; licenses unchanged.
- **Main files:** `extensions/*/` (22 packs), `extensions/internal/langpack/`, `extensions/all.go`, `extension/extension.go`, `internal/core/language/{registry.go,packfs/,languagetest/}`, `internal/core/settings`, `internal/gui/exthost/{languages,host,scope}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{process,workspacemodel}.go`, `cmd/emetos/languagepacks_test.go`, `scripts/update-assets.sh`, `NOTICE`, `docs/SPEC.md` D13, §5, §8.1, §10.15.

## 11.7 Window tab extensions

- **Date:** 2026-10-26

- **Summary:** SPEC §10.15, §10.14. `Contributes.WindowTabKinds` (`ID`, `Title`, `Icon`, `PlusMenuLabel`), the activation event `onWindowTab:<kind>`, `api.WindowTabs().Provide(kind, {Create(tab, state)})` with content `Build`, `SaveState`, `OnClose` and a handle `SetTitle`, `SetHint`, `StateChanged` (debounced save), `Refresh`, `Close`; the scope kind `ScopeWindowTab` holding only the owner, named `<extension id>:<tab id>`. An extension window tab is a `Workspace` with no folder whose shell shows only the content (`workbench.Options.EditorOnly`); it has the kind's icon and title in the strip, is listed by the `+` menu ("Workspace..." then one entry per kind of every enabled extension), the tray menu, the switch picker and the tab keys, has its own dispatcher, Help and Commands, and is saved in `workspaceTabs` with its title and state (restored in order). `emetos.windowTab.open` ("New Window Tab..."). A status bar item from a window tab is refused (`ExtensionStatusBarUnavailable`). Containment: a panic in `Create`, `OnClose` or `Build` fails only the owner with "<Name> failed"; a `Create` error leaves the error text in the tab. `extensions/scratchpad`: the Scratchpad window tab (notes saved in the tab's state across restarts; a "Recent edits" feed of `emetos.document.changed` from other tabs, which asks for the link on the first event). Dev commands `dev.openWindowTab` and `dev.openPlusMenu` (SPEC §15.2).

## 12.2 The JSON-RPC and LSP client core

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-12.2-lsp-client
- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12 "The client", D6, D20. `internal/core/lsp`: `Content-Length` framing (CRLF headers, 64 MiB body limit, any framing error ends the connection), JSON-RPC over any `io.ReadWriteCloser` with one reader, one writer draining an unbounded FIFO (so `Notify` never blocks and a `didChange` is written before a later request), notifications delivered in order on one goroutine and server requests answered concurrently; cancellation from the caller's `ctx` (`$/cancelRequest`, late responses dropped, no timeout of its own); `NewClient` does `initialize`/`initialized` with only the capabilities area 3 builds, negotiates the position encoding (offers UTF-8 and UTF-16, understands UTF-32) and keeps the server's capabilities and dynamic registrations; `Shutdown` (`shutdown`, `exit`, close); handlers for every server-to-client request and notification of §9.12 with defined defaults and error-code mapping; byte columns ⇄ UTF-8/16/32 conversion with clamping rules; document sync from the document model's change events (incremental, last edit first with ranges in the old text, or full; CRLF kept; versions; `willSave`, `willSaveWaitUntil`, `didSave` with text when asked; rename and Save As close and reopen; dynamic registration honoured); percent-encoded URIs; the typed messages of area 3 written by hand; a trace callback for step 12.3. `internal/core/lsp/lsptest`: a scripted fake server (answers, delays, sequences, messages it sends, crash and stall) that records the traffic, rebuilds each document counting characters as a server of its encoding does, and lists protocol violations, run in process over pipes or as the real `fakeserver` program in its own process group. SPEC §5.2 lists `lsp/`, `lsp/lsptest/` and `langfeatures/` (created in 12.3).
- **Main files:** `internal/core/lsp/{frame,queue,conn,client,sync,position,uri,capabilities,methods,types,types_features}.go`, `internal/core/lsp/lsptest/{script,server,harness}.go`, `internal/core/lsp/lsptest/fakeserver/main.go`, `docs/SPEC.md` §5.2, §9.12.
- **Tests:** about 50 top-level tests: framing (byte-at-a-time, partial and joined reads, bad and duplicate headers, wrong charset, over the limit, truncation, failing reader), matching (out of order, null, server errors), cancellation (sent, already-done `ctx` sends nothing, deadlines, server-side cancel), server requests and every error mapping, ordered notifications with a slow handler, connection failures (death mid-request and in the handshake, broken stream, write failure, close failure), the handshake (params from the recorded traffic, encoding negotiation, four failure modes), shutdown, every handler and default, positions (ASCII, 2/3/4-byte characters in three encodings, mid-character, line ends, out of range, round trip, invalid UTF-8), sync (open/close/reopen, incremental ranges in three encodings, multi-cursor order, undo/redo, `SetText`, line-ending change, CRLF, versions, rename and Save As, untitled, will save, did save text, unwanted methods not sent, dynamic registration, use after death), every wire-type shape with routes; the property test `TestRandomEditsKeepTheServerInSync` (5 server kinds × LF/CRLF × 6 seeds × 150 operations of edits, undo, redo, `SetText` and line-ending changes on mixed 1–4-byte text; the server's copy, version and violations checked; it catches a reversed edit order, checked by mutation). 7 subtests run the real `fakeserver` program. No existing test changed.

All decisions are in SPEC §9.12 "As built (step 12.2)".

## 12.3 Language server contributions and their lifetime

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-12.3-language-servers
- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12 "Servers and their lifetime", "Providers in Go", "Features model", §10.15. `Contributes.LanguageServers` (`ID`, `Name`, `Languages`, `Program`, `Args`, `Env`, `PathSetting`, `InitializationOptions`, `ConfigurationSection`, `WatchedFiles`, `OnActivation`; helpers for the qualified id, the trace setting and the restart command); the Go provider API `api.Languages().Register{Completion,Hover,SignatureHelp,Definition,Reference,DocumentSymbol,DocumentHighlight,CodeAction,Formatting,Rename}Provider` with its own types (zero-based line and byte column), every call guarded (`ExtensionProviderPanicked` fails the extension in that tab only, `ExtensionProviderFailed` does not). `lsp.StartProcess` (own process group, stderr lines, group kill). The host starts a server per tab and extension activation on the first document of its languages or at activation, attaches documents opened before it was ready, follows closes and renames, restarts after a crash (the fifth in 3 minutes gives up with a notification offering Restart and Show Output), stops it with `shutdown`/`exit` within 3 s then TERM and KILL, and reports a missing program once with Open Settings (starting when the path setting changes); the Output channel named after the server (log messages, stderr, show message, `<ext>.<id>.trace.server` off/messages/verbose); a status bar item while starting and during `$/progress`; `showMessage` as notifications and `showMessageRequest` as a sticky notification whose buttons answer; `workspace/configuration` from the section (`Store.Section`) with `didChangeConfiguration`; `didChangeWatchedFiles` from the watcher for the manifest globs and dynamic registrations; `<ext>.restartServer` in the palette from install; `workspace/applyEdit`. `core/langfeatures`: Emetos-side types, the per-tab registry with selectors and scores, VS Code's merge rules, a `ServerProvider` over the client (capability gated, keeps working across restarts), LSP conversions, and `Apply(ctx, host, edit)` (one undo step per document, text-only edits all or nothing, file operations in order through the window's file operations with the trash, a closed file opened as an unsaved document in an inactive editor). `extensions/golang` declares `gopls` (`go.goplsPath`, section `go.gopls`).

## 12.4 Diagnostics

- **Date:** 2026-10-26

- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12 "Diagnostics", §9.4, §9.5, §10.13. Pushed diagnostics go through an ordered, coalescing queue per server and a worker that drops publications older than the open document's version, converts ranges (from the document, or the file on disk for closed files) and sets Problems markers owned by the qualified server id; pull diagnostics (`textDocument/diagnostic` with `previousResultId`, `unchanged` reports, related documents, 250 ms after a change, `workspace/diagnostic/refresh`, dynamic registration). Markers become editor decorations in a new owner slot, so squiggles move with edits until the server republishes: VS Code's ranges (a hint is two characters, an empty range takes the word), a zigzag for error, warning and info and three dots for hints in the theme's colors, `unnecessary` faded (`editorUnnecessaryCode.opacity`, `editor.showUnused`), `deprecated` struck (`editor.showDeprecated`), only visible rows painted, none in read-only editors. The overview ruler and the minimap mark them. A hover model with participants (the diagnostic one: message, `source(code)` with the code's link, related information, View Problem; the seams for 12.6's providers and 12.8's quick fixes), VS Code's timing (delay, hiding delay, sticky, pinned; `editor.hover.*`). `editor.action.marker.next`/`prev` (F8/Shift+F8, across files with wrap) and `nextInFiles`/`prevInFiles` (Alt+F8/Shift+Alt+F8, in the file), showing the problem in a pinned hover closed by Escape; Next/Previous Problem in the Go menu. `dev.showHover`. Fixed the fake server recording its own control requests (its traffic answers doubled in size with every poll).

## Fix: Glutter 1.8.1

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** fix/glutter-1.8 (not a step of the plan)
- **Summary:** Emetos moves to Glutter v1.8.1 and drops the workarounds the new version makes unneeded. Font fallback (Glutter 28.16) replaces `kit/fonts/controls.go`, the controls font that joined Inter and the symbol font, and simplifies the editor's font families. `TreeSliverNode.SetExpanded`/`SetChildren` (28.17) let `kit.Tree` update its nodes in place instead of rebuilding the tree on every change. `widgets.NewErrorBoundary` (28.20) replaces `ui.NewBuilder`/`ui.BoxScope` (`extension/ui/box.go` is deleted): the box of an extension view puts a boundary keyed by the instance around the extension's widget, so a panic anywhere below it fails the extension with `ExtensionViewWidgetPanicked`. WindowDecoration's drag area (28.18) replaces the title bar's gesture-swallowing detectors: the menu and the workspace tabs are now `Leading`, and the empty title moves the window. This needed a new workaround, because `Leading` gets no width limit; it is the only entry left in `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`. `scripts/proof.sh` and `scripts/perf.sh` run headless with `GLUTTER_RENDERER=gles` unless the caller sets it. Also fixed: two flaky app tests, autosave (now waits for the dialog to show) and Save As while closing (now waits until the close is no longer busy).
- **Main files:** `go.mod`, `internal/gui/kit/{tree,fonts/fonts}.go`, `internal/gui/editor/text.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/box.go`, `internal/gui/workbench/titlebar.go`, `scripts/{proof,perf}.sh`, `docs/{SPEC,PERFORMANCE,GLUTTER-ISSUES}.md`.
- **Tests:** tree updates in place (expanded state kept, children replaced); a widget panic below an extension's view, and a re-enabled extension getting a fresh boundary (`exthost/views_test.go`); font fallback in the editor's text; the flaky closing and autosave tests (100 runs, and 20 with `-race` under load). No golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · release build ✅ · display tests none.
- **Measurements:** `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` "Results after Glutter 1.8". Memory is 422 MB without the old workaround. Warm cold start went from 920 to 340 ms, and opening a 10 MB file from 1000 to 470 ms. A restored 4-tab session uses 30–46 MB more; this is not chased yet, because the renderer change confounds the measurement.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** four removed (the Vulkan try headless, now avoided with `GLUTTER_RENDERER=gles` as Glutter's owner advises; the font loading for a missing character; tree node updates; the error boundary); the title bar entry was replaced by the `Leading` width one (still so in 1.8.1).

## 12.6 Hover and signature help

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-12.6-hover-signature
- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12 "Hover" and "Signature help".
  - **Hover:** VS Code's `HoverOperation` timing (H = `editor.hover.delay`): providers are asked at H/2, results show at H with the diagnostics part from 12.4, later answers are added, and "Loading..." appears at 3H. There is one operation per word (a diagnostic's range, else the word, else the character) and no request over white space or past the end of the line. `Registry.HoverEach` runs off the UI thread and hands each answer back as it resolves; answers for a replaced operation or an older document version are dropped, and the requests are cancelled with the operation. Parts stack diagnostics first, then the providers in order. The sticky hover keeps the `hidingDelay` grace. The box is at most 500 px wide and as tall as max(a quarter of the editor, 250), and scrolls. Markdown uses a new `Options.Hover` look of `extension/ui/markdown`, with code blocks colored by the fenced language's grammar and the theme (`SuggestHost.Tokenize`). Ctrl+K Ctrl+I shows the hover and, pressed again, focuses it; the eight scroll commands, `editor.action.hideHover`, and the `editorHoverVisible`/`editorHoverFocused` keys are added.
  - **Signature help:** a port of VS Code's `ParameterHintsModel`. It triggers on trigger characters after 120 ms, or at once with Ctrl+Shift+Space. Retrigger characters, typing and cursor moves ask again while the hints show, and requests carry `isRetrigger` and `activeSignatureHelp`. The hints close on Escape, blur, a mouse press, an empty answer or the setting turned off. Superseded requests are cancelled and stale answers dropped. Overloads switch with Up/Down (and Alt+Up/Down) or the arrows; the counter is "1/3", and `editor.parameterHints.cycle` makes the list wrap. The active parameter, found by the server's offsets or VS Code's whole-word rule, is drawn bold in `editorHoverWidget.highlightForeground`. Parameter and signature documentation are Markdown. The hints coexist with the suggest widget (Escape closes suggest first). New: `editor.parameterHints.enabled`/`cycle`, 4 commands, 3 context keys and `dev.triggerParameterHints`; `dev.showHover` now asks the providers too.
- **Main files:** `internal/gui/editor/{signature,signature_widget,hover,hover_widget,suggest,viewmodel,options,commands,input,pointer,widget,dev}.go`, `internal/core/langfeatures/{types,registry,merge,server,convert}.go`, `extension/ui/markdown/view.go`, `internal/gui/app/{suggest,devcommands}.go`, `internal/core/{command,keybinding,settings,theme}`, `internal/gui/kit/theme.go`, `testdata/proof/12.6/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §9.12.
- **Tests:**
  - `langfeatures`: `HoverEach` order and cancel, the `Has*` checks, signature characters, `LabelRange` and byte offsets, the signature context on the fake server.
  - `editor/hover_providers_test.go`: timing at H/2, H and 3H on the fake clock, stacking, stale answers, cancel, white space, the command, focus and scroll, sticky.
  - `editor/signature_test.go`: ports `parameterHintsModel.test.ts` (triggers, retriggers, close rules, cancel and drop, overloads with and without cycle, keys, active parameter).
  - `editor/hover_markdown_widget_test.go`: goldens, scrolling, links, placement.
  - `app/keys_hover_test.go`: five states for the default keys test.
  - `TestGoplsAnswersHoverAndSignatureHelp`: runs the real `gopls`.
  - Existing tests changed: the editor option defaults, and the keys test's states and reset.
  - Goldens: new `hover_markdown_{dark,light}`, `parameter_hints_{dark,light}`, `parameter_hints_overloads_{dark,light}`. No existing golden changed. The agent viewed them all, and I viewed two of them and both proofs.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · display tests none.

They are in the Go menu and the editor context menu (with a Peek submenu). One result jumps and records history, as a preview editor for another file. Several results follow `editor.gotoLocation.multiple*` (default `peek`). The alternative command runs when the cursor is already on the only result, and no result shows "No definition found".

The embedded editor shares an open document or reads the file from disk without a tab, and is coloured by its grammar. Keys: F4/Shift+F4, Up/Down, Enter, Ctrl+Enter, Escape. A peek view's editor never opens another.
  - **Highlights:** document highlights by kind (read, write, text) replace the word occurrences when a provider answers.

## 12.8 Code actions, formatting and rename

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-12.8-code-actions
- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12 "Code actions", "Formatting", "Rename", §10.11.
  - **Code actions:** VS Code's `getCodeActions` (hierarchical kinds, include, exclude and preferred filters, the `source.*` gate, VS Code's order, disabled actions kept apart).
    - **Light bulb:** asked 250 ms after the cursor rests or the diagnostics change, with the server's raw diagnostic (`Marker.Data`); it follows `editor.lightbulb.enabled` and uses the auto-fix icon for a preferred fix.
    - **List:** Ctrl+., Ctrl+Shift+R, Shift+Alt+O, Shift+Alt+. and the context menu, with headers, Show Disabled, key hints and Ctrl+Enter to preview. Quick Fix also asks at the nearest diagnostic of the line, and the diagnostic hover has "Quick Fix...".
    - **Applying:** resolve when needed, then the edit through `langfeatures.Apply` with every editor's cursors following and one undo step, then the command.
  - **Save participants:** they run before the write, in VS Code's order: trim, `editor.codeActionsOnSave` (`source.fixAll` first, `explicit`/`always`/`never` and the legacy forms), format on save, then the final newline. Each participant is bounded by `editor.codeActionsOnSaveTimeout`/`formatOnSaveTimeout` (750 ms); a timeout shows a silent notification and the save goes on. The participants' edits are one undo step. Auto save skips them, and Save without Formatting skips all. A save with no participants does no extra work.
  - **Formatting:** Format Document (Ctrl+Shift+I) and Format Selection (Ctrl+K Ctrl+F); a range-only formatter serves Format Document. VS Code's `computeMoreMinimalEdits` makes one undo step. Format on type and on paste, `editor.defaultFormatter`, and format on save in modes `file`, `modifications` and `modificationsIfAvailable`. The `modifications` modes compare with the file on disk, since there is no SCM.
  - **Rename:** F2 asks `prepareRename`, and a refusal shows its message. The box over the symbol takes Enter to apply across files and Ctrl+Enter for the refactor preview: a panel with a check box per file and per change, which applies only what is checked.
  - **Fixed on the way:** disposing an editor with a widget open notified a listener on an inactive element.
- **Main files:**
  - core: `internal/core/langfeatures/{codeaction,format,edit,merge,registry,server}.go`, `internal/core/{settings,command,keybinding}/codeactions.go`;
  - editor: `internal/gui/editor/{codeaction,codeaction_widget,format,rename,rename_widget,refactor_preview,saveparticipants}.go`;
  - app and exthost: `internal/gui/app/{saveformat,save,lsphost}.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/diagnostics.go`;
  - other: `testdata/proof/12.8/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §9.12, §6.3.
- **Tests:**

The real `gopls` test resolves and applies a quick fix, organizes imports, formats and renames across 3 files.
  - changed: the menus test, the editor options defaults, and the keys test.

## 12.9 Code lens, inlay hints, semantic tokens, folding and selection ranges

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-12.9-lens-hints-tokens
- **Summary:** SPEC §9.12, §8.6, §9.2.
  - **Code lenses:** a one-line view-zone row above their line, with the titles of a line joined by " | ". They are resolved when their row comes into view, and a click runs the command. Requests are debounced, and stale answers are dropped by generation and version. Settings: `editor.codeLens`, `codeLensFontFamily`, `codeLensFontSize`.
  - **Inlay hints:** injected into the row's paragraph only, with one map between document and drawn offsets. The document, clipboard, find and undo never see them, and the cursor never rests inside a hint. They support the four `editor.inlayHints.enabled` modes with Ctrl+Alt, padding, `maximumLength` (43, ellipsis), `fontSize` and `fontFamily`, and the `editorInlayHint.*` colours. Label parts have tooltips, and Ctrl+click on a part opens its location or runs its command. Requests cover the viewport plus 30 lines.
  - **Semantic tokens:**
    - a per-window cache with full, delta (`ApplySemanticEdits`) and range requests while a full one is pending, and `workspace/semanticTokens/refresh`;
    - decoding in UTF-16 (UTF-8 and UTF-32 servers converted);
    - styling with VS Code's ported `parseTokenSelector` scoring, the theme's `semanticTokenColors`, `editor.semanticTokenColorCustomizations` rules, and the type and modifier fallback to TextMate scopes, drawn over the TextMate spans;
    - tokens move with edits until the next answer;
    - cost per key: about 0.26 ms for a letter and 0.57 ms for a line break, with 100,000 tokens over 20,000 lines;
    - new error tags: `SemanticTokensEditInvalid`, `SemanticTokensDeltaUnexpected`.
  - **Folding ranges:** the server's ranges replace the indentation provider (`editor.foldingStrategy`), cleaned by a port of `sanitizeRanges` (kinds, `foldingMaximumRegions`, crossing ranges dropped). They support `collapsedText` and `editor.foldingImportsByDefault`.
  - **Selection ranges:** Expand Selection asks `textDocument/selectionRange` once for all cursors, and falls back to the editor's own ranges.
  - **gopls:** inlay hints and semantic tokens are switched on through the `go.gopls` settings (`semanticTokens`, `hints`).
- **Main files:**
  - core: `internal/core/langfeatures/{lens,ranges,semantic,registry,server}.go`, `internal/core/folding/{syntax,provider,regions}.go`, `internal/core/theme/{semantic,resolve,theme}.go`, `internal/core/settings/lens.go`;
  - editor and app: `internal/gui/editor/{codelens,inlay,inlay_interact,semantic,selectionranges,lensoptions,geometry,render,folding,viewmodel}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{editors,workspacemodel}.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/servers.go`;
  - other: `testdata/proof/12.9/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §9.12.
- **Tests:**
  - added:
    - core: conversion and resolve of lenses and hints, folding answers and `Sanitize`, semantic decoding and delta edits with both tag routes, the cache, theme selectors, scoring, fallback and flag merge.
    - editor: lens rows and click; hints that leave the text unchanged, cursor skipping, the four modes, padding and truncation, hover and click, stale answers, a settings change; `moveTokens` and `mergeSemantic` tables and the request flow; server folding against the fallback; the selection range expansion.
    - `TestGoplsAnswersHintsTokensFoldingAndSelectionRanges` runs the real `gopls`: 15 hints, 68 tokens, folding, selection ranges and the `//go:generate` lens.
  - changed: span literals in two editor tests, an argument in a hit-test test, and the options defaults.
- **Goldens:**
  - new: `semantic_tokens_{dark,light}_modern`.
  - changed: `settings_font_{dark,light}`, where a search for "font" now finds the four new font settings.
  - The agent viewed them all; I viewed the dark semantic golden and the proof.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/12.9-hints-tokens-lens.png`, made with `scripts/proof.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/12.9-hints-tokens-lens.png --config testdata/proof/12.9/config --after 9s --commands '[["dev.openFile",{"path":"testdata/proof/12.9/project/main.go","line":20,"column":0}],["sleep","8s"]]' -- testdata/proof/12.9/project`. The real `gopls` shows the code lens "run go generate ./... | run go generate", parameter, type and field hints, and semantic colours.

  ![12.9 hints, tokens and lens](implemented-proof-images/12.9-hints-tokens-lens.png)

The agent's first race run failed about 150 app tests, because a theme change notified listeners during a build; listeners are now told after the frame. ## 13.1 The `claude` adapter's protocol

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-13.1-agents
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.16. This step adds `extensions/agents/claude`, a Go client of the `claude` program's stream-json mode, with no UI.
  - **Finding the program:** `Find` reads `agents.claude.path`, else `PATH`.
  - **Login status:** `AuthStatus(ctx)` reads `claude auth status`, using the exit status as the answer and keeping the JSON's method, provider, email, organization and subscription.
  - **Starting:** `Adapter.Start` runs the §10.16 command line in the workspace folder, in its own process group:
    ```
    -p --input-format stream-json --output-format stream-json --verbose --include-partial-messages --replay-user-messages --permission-prompt-tool stdio --permission-mode <mode> [--session-id|--resume] [--model]
    ```
    It does the `initialize` handshake before it returns. The environment is the user's, unchanged, plus `CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_SDK_FILE_CHECKPOINTING=true`: never `--bare`, never `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, never a key or token.
  - **Messages:** typed `system/init` (with capabilities), `assistant` (text, thinking, `tool_use`), `user` (`tool_result`, replays), `stream_event` (partial text joined per parent tool use), and `result`. Unknown types keep their raw JSON.
  - **Control channel:**
    - out: `interrupt`, `set_permission_mode`, `set_model`, `rewind_files`;
    - in: `can_use_tool`, one goroutine per request; a cancel request ends its `ctx`;
    - request IDs with a 60 s timeout, and a dead program fails every pending request.
  - **Events:** delivered in order through an unbounded queue, so a slow UI never blocks the reading.
  - **Lines:** up to 16 MiB; a bad line becomes an event and the stream goes on.
  - **Ending:** `Close` closes stdin, then TERM and KILL to the group.
  - **Error tags:** 16, all route-tested.
  - **Sources of the wire shapes:** the Agent SDK documentation and the Python SDK, read online, not copied.
- **Main files:** `extensions/agents/claude/{command,auth,lines,messages,partial,control,process,session}.go`, `extensions/agents/claude/internal/fakeclaude/main.go`, `extensions/agents/claude/claudetest/`, `extensions/agents/claude/testdata/*.jsonl` (17 fixtures), `docs/SPEC.md` §10.16, `docs/MANUAL-CHECKS.md`, `docs/IMPLEMENTATION.md` (13.3's rewind wording).
- **Tests:** everything runs against a fake `claude` that the tests build. It replays the JSONL fixtures, answers control requests, and records its argv, environment and process group. `TestMain` makes `PATH` an empty folder, moves `HOME` and `XDG_*`, and unsets `CLAUDE*`/`ANTHROPIC*`, so the real program can't be reached. Covered:
  - parsing: every message type (a table of fixtures) and partial text;
  - the command line, environment, working folder and process group;
  - permissions: the `can_use_tool` round trip (allow with `updatedInput` and `updatedPermissions`, deny, handler error, nil handler, cancel);
  - the control channel: interrupt, mode, model, rewind, timeouts, a bad handshake;
  - failures: a dead program, malformed lines, a slow `OnEvent`;
  - the auth status cases;
  - processes: cancel and `Close` kill the process group, including a program that ignores TERM.

  `real_test.go` (tag `emetos_claude`) is compiled, not run.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** none; this step has no UI. The test logs of the fake program are the proof.
- **Manual checks:** withdrawn: the extension was removed in step 17c.1 (D26) before the check was run, and the check left docs/MANUAL-CHECKS.md.
- **Glutter issues:** none
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.16 "As built (step 13.1)" lists 10 decisions.
  - `--permission-prompt-tool stdio` is added to the command line, as the Python SDK does when a permission callback is set.
  - `CLAUDECODE` is passed through unchanged; the Python SDK removes it (decision 74).
  - Rewind is offered on every session Emetos started, because no capability names it, and a refusal shows the program's reason. Step 13.3's tasks and tests now say so.
  - No streams recorded from the real program: that needs the user's account, so the fixtures are written from the documented shapes.

## 13.2 Sessions: the side bar view, the session tab and the title action

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-13.2-agent-sessions
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.16, §10.15. The `agents` extension, registered in `extensions/all.go`.
  - **Side bar:** an "Agents" container with a Sessions view. Its index is the view's state, per workspace. Rows are newest first, with the name or the first message and how long ago. A row can be renamed inline, deleted (its tabs and program close too) or clicked to open or resume the session, and there is a New Session button.
  - **Session tab:**
    - the conversation, streamed: events are applied once per frame, and the view follows the end only while you are there;
    - thinking folded, tool calls as rows whose results unfold, sub-agent calls indented, Markdown with coloured code;
    - the input box: Enter sends, Shift+Enter makes a new line, `/` completes the handshake's commands, `@` completes files;
    - Stop, and the permission mode and model pickers.
  - **Title action:** the `hubot` icon, "New Session (runs Claude Code)".
  - **Add Selection to Agent:** Alt+K and the editor context menu insert `@path#Lstart-end`. `agents.attachActiveFile` adds the open file to each message. A built-in deny list (`.env`, keys) and the project's `.claude/settings*.json` `Read(...)` deny rules apply; the user's home files are never read.
  - **Log in:** `claude auth login` in a pseudo-terminal panel, "Agents: Log in". The missing, logged-out and failed states are banners with a button, and a tab waiting for login starts once it succeeds.
  - **Restore:** session tabs restore with their conversation, pickers and handshake, and start no program until the next message, then use `--resume`. A closed session keeps the last 48 KiB of its conversation for the 20 most recent sessions.
  - **Permissions:** every `can_use_tool` request is denied with "Emetos can't answer this permission request yet." until step 13.3 (the seam is `sessionTab.canUseTool`).
  - **New in the shared packages:** `ui.Dropdown`, `ui.ScrollView`, `ui.ScrollHandle`, `markdown.Options.Flow`, and `claude.ParseLine`; the fake program has an `auth login` mode.
  - **Dev command:** `dev.agents.replay` replays a recorded conversation with no program, in debug builds only.
  - **New error tags:** 11, all route-tested.
- **Main files:** `extensions/agents/{agents,list,session,transcript,tabview,completion,context,login,pty,dev}.go`, `extensions/agents/claude/{messages.go,internal/fakeclaude/main.go,claudetest/claudetest.go}`, `extension/ui/{ui.go,markdown/view.go}`, `internal/gui/kit/scrollable_element.go`, `extensions/all.go`, `testdata/proof/13.2/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.16, §15.2.
- **Tests:** these run the real adapter against the fake program, and every test that starts a program checks from the fake's log that its process group is gone. Covered:
  - a new session streams into the tab; the list renames, resumes and deletes;
  - two tabs at once; Stop; `/` and `@` completion, unit and through the keys;
  - Add Selection with its deny rules, and the attached file;
  - the missing and logged-out states and the login flow;
  - restore without a start, then `--resume`;
  - a dead program; the denied permission; replay; the pty.

  No existing test or golden changed.
- **Goldens:**
  - `extensions/agents`: `session_{dark,light}`, `session_working`, `banner_{missing,loggedout,failed}`, `completion_commands`;
  - `extension/ui/markdown`: `flow_{dark,light}`.

  The agent viewed them all; I viewed the missing banner, the completion and the proof.

## 13.3 Permissions, proposed edits, plans and checkpoints

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-13.3-agent-permissions
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.16, §9.11, §10.15.
  - **`Editors().OpenProposedChange`** is new in `extension/`. It takes `ProposedChange{URI, Original, Proposed, Title, Language}` and returns `ProposedResult{Accepted, Text, Edited}`. It blocks and takes a `ctx`; cancelling closes the tab.
    - The diff opens beside the active group, with an editable right side made of virtual documents that are never dirty, saved or backed up. Accept and Reject are in its title, and closing it in any way rejects.
    - Emetos never writes the proposed text; the program applies the edit after `allow`.
  - **Edits:**
    - `Edit`: `old_string` is replaced in the file's text, with `replace_all` and CRLF handled. Impossible edits are denied at once with the reason.
    - `Write`: the new content against the current file, or an empty one.
    - `NotebookEdit`: the cell's source.
    - An edited Accept sends `updatedInput`. For `Edit`, that is a minimal unique `old_string`/`new_string` that reproduces the user's text exactly.
    - Files outside the folder, excluded by a deny rule or unreadable go to the dialog.
  - **The panel above the input:**
    - the tool dialog (the command, the URL or the input, and the reason), with Allow once, Allow for this session (the request's suggestions with the `session` destination), and Deny with a message;
    - `ExitPlanMode` (the plan as Markdown, with Approve, which returns the mode to Manual, and Keep Planning);
    - `AskUserQuestion` (options, an own answer, Answer or Skip).

    Requests queue in order (the adapter adds `ToolRequest.Seq`), and a request the program withdraws closes its dialog or diff without an answer.
  - **Rewind code to here:** under each user message while the program runs and is idle. It asks for confirmation first, then shows the result or the program's reason.
  - **Hidden tabs:** a notification with "Show" appears when a turn ends, or a request waits, in a hidden tab. A waiting tab gets the dot.
  - **Status item** `agents.status`: "N waiting, M working"; a click goes to the session.
  - **Output channel "Agents":** the adapter's log, the program's stderr, and the extension's decisions.
  - **Replay:** `dev.agents.replay` now shows the requests of a recording.
  - **New error tags:** 15, all route-tested.
- **Main files:** `extensions/agents/{permissions,permview,edits,rewind,status,session,tabview,dev}.go`, `extension/editors.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/editors.go`, `internal/gui/app/{exproposed,exttitle}.go`, `internal/core/editorgroup/diffinput.go`, `extensions/agents/claude/{control,session}.go`, `extensions/internal/fakeapi/`, `testdata/proof/13.3/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.16.
- **Tests:** run with the fake program, plus the host and app tests.
  - Edits: Accept, an edited Accept (applying `updatedInput` gives the user's text), Reject, close, a reason typed, an impossible edit, and the dialog fallback; Write.
  - Dialogs: the Bash dialog's three answers through taps; a plan approved and kept; a question answered and skipped.
  - Requests: queued requests, withdrawn requests, and a closing tab.
  - Rewind: confirm, cancel, the result, and a refusal.
  - The notification, mark, status item and Output channel.
  - Host and app: the host API (open, accept, edited, reject, close, cancel, deactivation, several at once); in the app, the file stays untouched in every case, plus CRLF and a new file.
  - Changed: the 13.2 test of the denial seam is removed with its fixture.
- **Goldens:** new `permission_bash_{dark,light}`, `permission_plan_dark`, `permission_question_dark`, `permission_edit_dark`, `rewind_button`, `rewind_question`. The agent viewed them all; I viewed the Bash dialog and the proof. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/13.3-proposed-edit.png`, made with the plan's command:
  ```
  scripts/proof.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/13.3-proposed-edit.png --config testdata/proof/13.3/config --commands '[["agents.newSession"],["dev.agents.replay",{"fixture":"testdata/proof/13.3/edit.jsonl"}]]' -- testdata/proof/13.3/project
  ```

  It shows the session tab with the Edit review panel, beside the diff of `main.go (proposed)` with Accept and Reject in its title, and "1 waiting" in the status bar.

  ![13.3 proposed edit](implemented-proof-images/13.3-proposed-edit.png)

  - `OpenProposedChange` takes `Original` and a `ctx`;
  - the diff opens beside the active group;

## 14.1 gopls, the status bar and the tools

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-14.1-go-extension
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.17, §9.12, §10.15. The extension `extensions/golang` is renamed "Go".
  - **Settings:** declared in a new `Manifest.Contributes.Settings`, whose keys may sit outside the extension id: `go.gopls` (one object, as in VS Code; its description lists VS Code Go's 43 gopls properties, from `gopls-settings.json`, which `update-assets.sh` copies from vscode-go v0.59.0), `go.alternateTools`, `go.toolsManagement.go`, `go.toolsGopath`, `go.toolsEnvVars`.
  - **Restart:** "Go: Restart Language Server", through the new `LanguageServerSpec.Category`.
  - **Server-state API:** `api.LanguageServers()` is new, with `Status`, `OnChange` (on the UI thread, once per burst), `Restart` and `ShowOutput`, plus `LanguageServerSpec.OwnUI`.
  - **Quick pick API:** `api.QuickPick().Show` is new.
  - **On save:** VS Code Go's `[go]` defaults (tabs, format on save, `source.organizeImports` on explicit saves) are in the Go pack, and the 12.8 save participants do the work.
  - **Status item:** shown while a Go file is in front.
    - The version comes from `go env GOVERSION`, run off the UI thread with `GOTOOLCHAIN=local` and a 20 s limit.
    - It shows gopls's state, starting, running (✓), working (with progress), stopped or not found, or a warning when `go` is missing.
    - Its menu: Restart, Show gopls Output, Install/Update Tools, Show Go Environment, Open Settings.
  - **"Go: Install/Update Tools":** installs `gopls`, `dlv` and `staticcheck` at `@latest` in the "Go Tools" panel. Each runs `go install` in its own process group and temporary folder, with the output streamed and a Cancel button. A summary notification follows, and gopls restarts if it was missing.
  - **Missing gopls:** one notification per activation, with Install.
  - **"Go: Show Go Environment":** `go env` in the Output channel "Go".
  - **New error tags:** 11, plus 3 test-helper tags, all route-tested.
- **Main files:** `extension/{quickpick,languageservers,manifest,api}.go`, `extensions/golang/{golang,env,run,settings,status,tools}.go`, `extensions/golang/gopls-settings.json`, `extensions/golang/assets/package.json`, `extensions/golang/gotest/` (the fake `go`), `internal/gui/exthost/{serverapi,servers,scope,api}.go`, `internal/gui/app/extensions.go`, `extensions/internal/fakeapi/servers.go`, `scripts/update-assets.sh`, `NOTICE`, `testdata/proof/14.1/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.17, §9.12, §10.15.
- **Tests:**

    - app: the quick input; a Go save organizes imports then formats in one undo step with the defaults alone;
    - `extensions/golang`, with a fake `go` built by `TestMain` and `PATH`, `HOME` and `XDG` isolated, so no test can reach a real `go install`: every status state and the menu, the install arguments, environment, failure and cancel, the missing tool, Show Go Environment;
    - tagged tests run the real `go env` (read-only) and the real gopls under the new declaration: 14 gopls-tagged tests pass, none skipped.
  - changed: the save-pipeline tests now put their settings in a `[go]` block, because the new language defaults beat plain settings, as in VS Code. The bare-scope test of `cmd/emetos` gets a workspace's services, and both of its extension tests run with an empty `PATH`.
- **Goldens:** new `tools_{idle,running,failed}_{dark,light}`, all viewed by the agent; I viewed the failed one and the proof. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · display tests none.

## 14.2 Go commands

- **Date:** 2026-10-26
- **Branch:** feat/step-14.2-go-commands
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.17, §10.15. The commands are `emetos.golang.*` in the palette (category "Go"). The editor context menu shows all but Run for Go files.
  - **Add Tags and Remove Tags:**
    - `go.addTags` and `go.removeTags` are registered with VS Code Go's defaults; the prompts use the new input box.
    - They act on the innermost struct at the cursor, or on the fields the selection touches.
    - They use gopls's `gopls.modify_tags` when it is announced. Otherwise a pure-Go port of gomodifytags (with structtag and camelcase) edits the tree, prints it with `go/format`, and applies the difference as one undo step.
  - **Fill Struct:** gopls's `refactor.rewrite.fillStruct` at the selection; with no gopls, a warning.
  - **Generate Unit Tests For Function:** a gotests-style table-driven skeleton for the function at the cursor (fields for the receiver, args, wants, `wantErr`), with the imports it needs.
    - A new test file is written with `O_EXCL`; an existing one gets the test and missing imports as one undo step.
    - A test of the same name is shown, not duplicated.
  - **Toggle Test File:** `foo.go` ↔ `foo_test.go`; a missing file is created with the package clause.
  - **Run:** "Go: Run" runs `go run .` in the "Go Run" panel, with the output streamed, Stop, Run Again and the exit status, in its own process group.
  - **New extension API:** `Documents().Apply`, `LanguageServers().Commands`, `ExecuteCommand`, `ApplyCodeAction`, and `InputBox().Show`. Server calls are asynchronous, with `done` on the UI thread.
  - **New error tags:** 13, plus 4 test-helper tags, all route-tested.
- **Main files:** `extensions/golang/{tags,tagscmd,fillstruct,gentest,toggletest,runpanel,editor,settings,golang}.go`, `extension/{inputbox,api,languageservers}.go`, `internal/gui/exthost/{inputbox,servercommands,api,scope}.go`, `internal/gui/app/extensions.go`, `extensions/internal/fakeapi/editing.go`, the fake `go` (now with `go run`), `third_party/gomodifytags/`, `NOTICE` (item 14), `testdata/proof/14.2/`, `docs/SPEC.md` §10.17, §10.15.
- **Tests:**

    - Fill Struct in 8 server states;
    - Toggle Test File: 6 cases;
    - the generator: 18 golden test files, plus duplicates and errors;
    - Run with the fake `go`: streaming, statuses, Stop killing the group, a new Run replacing the old, the end of the activation;
    - `exthost` with the fake LSP server: commands, `ExecuteCommand` with an `applyEdit`, `ApplyCodeAction` in 10 cases;
    - the input box.
  - tagged `emetos_gopls`: the real gopls `modify_tags` agrees with the pure-Go editor on 24 of 26 cases (the 2 differences are documented and asserted), real Fill Struct, real `go run`; 22 tests pass, none skipped.

## 14.3 Tests, lint and vet

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-14.3-go-tests-lint
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.17, §10.13.
  - **Code lenses for extensions:** a new API, `Languages().RegisterCodeLensProvider` and `RefreshCodeLenses`, with `extension.NewCommand` and `CodeLens.Disabled` (drawn at half opacity, not clickable).
    - The Go extension puts lenses in `*_test.go` files, found with `go/parser` by go test's rules: "run test | debug test" (and the benchmark form), and "run package tests | run file tests" on the package line.
    - "debug" is disabled until area 6; `go.enableCodeLens.runtest` turns the lenses off.
  - **Commands:** Test Function At Cursor, Benchmark Function At Cursor, Test File, Test Package, Test Previous, Cancel Running Tests, Vet Current Package, Vet Workspace, Lint Current Package, Lint Workspace.
    - Tests run `go test -json -timeout <go.testTimeout> <go.testFlags> -run ^(A|B)$` in local-directory mode, so reruns are never cached, in its own process group. A new run stops the old one.
  - **"Go Tests" panel:** a tree of packages, tests and subtests with pass, fail and skip, durations, and each node's output, streamed as the JSON events come. Panics and build failures show as failed packages. It has Stop and Run Again.
  - **Problems:** test failures (`file:line`, build errors, the first panic frame in the workspace), `go vet`, and the lint tool, each under its own owner, replacing only its own scope.
  - **On save:** `go.vetOnSave` (`package`) and `go.lintOnSave` (`package`) with `go.lintTool` (staticcheck by default; golangci-lint v1 and v2, revive), debounced 400 ms, cancelling an older run. A missing staticcheck offers Install once.

The editor package passes 20 times without and 5 times with `-race`.

## 15.1 The protocol and the headless view

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-15.1-remotework
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.18.
  - **Protocol** (`extensions/remotework/protocol`, version 1, magic `EMRW`): a message is `type:1 | length:4 | payload`, and each of the 13 types (Hello, Tile, FrameConfirm, Refresh, Key, Mouse, Scroll, Clipboard, Notification, NotificationAction, Resize, Ping, Disconnect) has its own limit. The largest is 1 MiB.
    - The length is checked against the type's limit before anything is read or allocated. An unknown type is refused, not skipped.
    - Payloads must match their sizes exactly, and fields are strictly validated (enums, UTF-8, finite floats, nonzero sizes), so the format is canonical: decoding then encoding gives the same bytes.
    - Encoding allocates nothing.
  - **Tile differ** (`extensions/remotework/tiles`): 64x64 tiles changed since the confirmed frame, each deflated alone at `flate.DefaultCompression`.
    - The host keeps only `maphash` hashes of the last 16 unconfirmed frames: 33 KB at 1280x800.
    - It does a full picture on request (Refresh), on an unknown confirm, and periodically.
    - The client's `Canvas` drops stale tiles and says when to confirm.
    - At 1280x800, a whole picture is 260 tiles, 113 KB and 7.8 ms; one typed character is 7 tiles and 6 KB.
  - **Headless view:** `api.Workspace().NewHeadlessView(size)` in `extension/headless.go`, implemented in `internal/gui/app`.
    - It is a second Glutter binding on the application's UI thread, with a window-less platform host and a workbench of its own.
    - The view has its own commands, focus, layout, dialogs, quick input, status bar and zoom. It shares documents, settings, theme, notifications, problems and file operations with the main window.
    - Pictures are rasterized with Glutter's software renderer and handed to `OnFrame` on a delivery goroutine, at most one frame per `max(33 ms, 2 x the last raster cost)`.
    - Keys, pointer, scroll and clipboard go in. `Resize` re-lays out the view; `Close`, deactivation, a folder change or the workspace closing end it.
    - The view's editors don't blink, so an idle view makes no frames.
    - Commands that act on Emetos itself (quit, tabs, new window) are not bound. Save As uses the quick input. Opening a link does nothing.
  - **Exthost:** the size is checked, the scope is asked (`ScopeOptions.NewHeadlessView`), and the view closes with the activation. `fakeapi` has a fake view.
  - **New error tags:** 23, all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - protocol: table round trips of every message, malformed and oversized input with routes, an allocation test, 20,000 seeded random inputs (the canonical property), and `FuzzDecode`.
  - tiles: round trips, a lossy-link simulation, stale tiles and confirms, refresh and reset, measurements on real editor pictures.
  - exthost and fakeapi: the size table, unavailable, closed with the activation.
  - app: about 20 tests (an edit in the main view shows in the headless view, typing, shortcuts, mouse, scroll, quick input, dialogs, zoom, resize, close on a folder change, an idle view makes no frames), and a benchmark.
- **Goldens:** new `headless_editor`, `headless_typed` and `headless_scrolled`. I viewed all three. They are skipped under `-race` (the software renderer is about 100 times slower there) and run in the normal and cgo-free runs. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `FuzzDecode` 30 s (`-parallel=1`, `GOMEMLIMIT=2GiB`) ✅ with no crashers · display tests none.
- **Proof:** the step has no UI of its own (the Remote Work tab is 15.4), so the proof is the `headless_typed` golden: the headless view of a workspace after `x` was typed into it. `docs/implemented-proof-images/15.1-headless-typed.png`.

  ![15.1 headless view](implemented-proof-images/15.1-headless-typed.png)
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** new in `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`: "No public way to draw a view off screen and to read its pixels". The workaround in `internal/gui/app/headless_engine.go` uses a `gluttertest.Tester` as the rasterizer, on the UI thread (rendering on another goroutine races in the text engine's shape cache), and replaces it every 8 frames. This puts `gluttertest` and `testing` in the editor binary (+0.7 MB; it registers no flags) and makes a 1280x800 picture cost about 85 ms of the UI thread.
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.18 "As built (step 15.1)" holds the wire table and the decisions:
  - fixed 4-byte lengths;
  - versions must be equal;
  - frames are relative to the confirmed picture;
  - the host keeps hashes, not pictures;
  - no caret blink in a view;
  - `NewHeadlessView` is not privilege-restricted (16.1's privileges cover events and commands only).

  Not built: extension contributions in the view, damage-limited rendering (needs Glutter), a view of a workspace that is not on screen. `TestHoverShowHoverCommandShowsFocusesAndHides` (editor) failed once under `-race` in a loaded full run and passed on every rerun; it is watched as a possible flake.

## 15.2 Login over SSH and the Remote Work page

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-15.2-ssh-login
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.18.
  - **Dependency:** `golang.org/x/crypto` v0.58.0 (`ssh`, `ssh/agent`), which brings in `golang.org/x/term` v0.47.0.
  - **`extensions/internal/sshlogin`** (shared with Relay):
    - **Server on A:** public keys only (no password, keyboard-interactive or none), checked against the list at every attempt and again after the signature is verified. The host key is Ed25519 only, and there are at most 5 tries per connection.
    - **Channels and requests:** one channel of the given type (`emetos-remotework`). Any other channel type, any channel request (shell, exec, pty, env, subsystem, forwarding) and any global request is refused.
    - **Deadlines:** 10 s to log in, 10 s to open the channel, at most 16 connections logging in at once.
    - **Rate limit:** 5 failures per sliding minute per address (an IPv6 address counts as its /64), checked at accept and bounded to 4096 addresses.
    - **Client on B:** an OpenSSH or PEM key file with up to 3 passphrase tries (bytes wiped after parsing), or an `ssh-agent` socket passed in by the caller.
    - **Host key pinning:** in `remote-work-hosts.json`. First use is asked with no connection open; a changed key is refused (`SSHLoginHostKeyChanged`) and sends nothing.
  - **`extensions/internal/statefile`:** atomic 0600 writes in a 0700 folder, and a checked read: a regular file within its size, not group- or world-writable, and a private key not readable by others.
  - **The hub** (`extensions/remotework`):
    - one SSH listener while any open workspace with a folder has Remote Work on;
    - one session per workspace, where a new login replaces the old one;
    - a 256-bit token in memory, and the grant carries the QUIC certificate fingerprint (empty until 15.3, meaning "SSH only");
    - the idle timeout;
    - the banner "Remote Work: <comment> connected" with Disconnect;
    - notifications for logins and refused attempts, at most one per address per minute.
  - **Login on B:** `extensions/remotework/login` (the request and grant over the channel, `Connect`) and `loginui` (the passphrase, first-use and changed-key dialogs for the 15.4 tab).
  - **The page "Remote Work"** (`emetos.remotework.manage`):
    - the per-workspace switch with the listener status and connected clients;
    - the port (47123) and the idle timeout (1–1440 minutes) with Apply;
    - the allowed keys with Remove, paste, "Choose .pub File...", and a place for 15.5's Scan QR Code;
    - the host fingerprint;
    - the security notes.

    An unsafe `remote-work.json` locks the page and leaves Remote Work off.
  - **New APIs:** `api.Banners()`, `api.FileChooser()`, and `InputBoxOpts.Obscure`, each with a fake.
  - **Scripts:** `scripts/isolated.sh` and `scripts/proof.sh` take `--state <dir>`.
  - **New error tags:** about 50 (`SSHLogin*`, `StateFile*`, `RemoteWork*`, `LoginUICancelled`, `ExtensionBanner*`, `ExtensionFileChooser*`), all route-tested.
- **Tests:** all on loopback with ephemeral ports and isolated `XDG_*` folders:
  - a listed key logs in;
  - refused: an unlisted key, passwords and keyboard-interactive, other channel types, shell and exec, a second channel, silence, too many connections logging in;
  - an in-process agent on a temp socket;
  - a wrong passphrase;
  - first-use pinning, a changed key, and `Forget`;
  - the rate limit (fake clock);
  - turning Remote Work off closes the listener and ends sessions;
  - a port change, the idle timeout, replace-on-relogin;
  - state file modes and the read policy (fifo, symlink, permissions);
  - the page, the dialogs and the banner in `gluttertest`.
- **Goldens:** new `page_{off,on_error}_{dark,light}`, `dialog_{passphrase,first_use,changed_key}_dark` and `banner_{dark,light}`. I viewed the changed-key and first-use dialogs, `banner_light`, `page_on_error_light` and the proof; the agent viewed all of them. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · display tests none.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/15.2-remote-work-page.png`, made with:
  ```
  scripts/proof.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/15.2-remote-work-page.png --config testdata/proof/15.2/config --state testdata/proof/15.2/state --commands '[["emetos.remotework.manage", null]]' --after 2s -- testdata/proof/15.2/project
  ```
