  ![15.2 remote work page](implemented-proof-images/15.2-remote-work-page.png)
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.18 "As built (step 15.2)" lists the decisions:
  - the host key is made when the page opens or Remote Work is first turned on;
  - one session per workspace, and a relogin replaces the old one;
  - a workspace is named by its folder name (the whole path when two share one), and a client names one when several are on;
  - `authorized_keys` options, certificates, DSA and RSA under 2048 bits are refused;
  - no "never" idle timeout;
  - the state folder is the default profile's;
  - the user's own key file permissions are not checked.

  15.3 plugs in through `HubOptions.Cert` and `HubOptions.OnSession`. Not built: QUIC (15.3), the B tab and reconnection (15.4), Scan QR Code (15.5).

## 15.3 QUIC transport and the TCP fallback

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-15.3-quic
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.18.
  - **Dependency:** `github.com/quic-go/quic-go` v0.64.1 (MIT), which adds `golang.org/x/net` v0.60.0 as an indirect dependency. `NOTICE` lists quic-go, plus `x/crypto` and `x/net`, which were missing.
  - **`extensions/remotework/transport`:** one `Conn` interface for both links.
    - `Send` is reliable and bounded (512 messages, 8 MiB). `SendTiles` is best effort, latest wins. `Next` gives messages, completed tiles and the peer's Disconnect. `Close` takes a reason.
    - `HostConn` is A's side and `ClientConn` is B's.
    - `Presenter` (A: the differ, at most 2 pictures in flight, a retry, the 5 s full refresh over QUIC only) and `Screen` (B: canvas, confirm, refresh request) carry the picture flow.
  - **Host (A):** QUIC on the UDP port with the SSH port's number, with a self-signed P-256 certificate made in memory at start.
    - The certificate's SHA-256 goes into the 15.2 grant. A hub whose UDP port cannot open runs over SSH.
    - B's first stream must be a Hello, checked by its header before the payload (at most 133 bytes before the token is known). The token is compared in constant time and looked up with `Hub.SessionByToken`.
    - A connection that stays silent for 5 s is closed, and at most 32 connections may wait for their Hello.
  - **Client (B):** TLS 1.3 only, with ALPN `emetos-remotework/1`. `VerifyConnection` accepts exactly one certificate whose SHA-256 matches, in constant time, and never consults system roots. A mismatch is fatal and does not fall back to SSH (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 75).
  - **Tiles as datagrams:** an encoded `Tile` is split into chunks of 1,100 bytes behind a 14-byte header (frame, index, total, fragment, fragments), and is counted only when every fragment has arrived.
    - At 1280x800, 86% of a whole picture's tiles fit one datagram, the median tile is 128 bytes, and the largest is 3 KB.
    - B follows only the newest picture. Reassembly is bounded to 256 partial tiles and 2 MiB.
    - After a quiet time, B asks for what is missing with a new message, **`Missing` (type 14)**: frame, total and a bitmap. It backs off and asks at most 8 times without progress. The protocol stays at version 1 (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 76).
  - **Reliable stream:** one bidirectional QUIC stream. A write stalled for 15 s drops the link.
  - **Resume:** a dropped QUIC connection is redialled with the token (3 tries of 3 s), queued messages survive, and Refresh goes first.
  - **Fallback:** no QUIC handshake within 3 s, a grant without a fingerprint, or a failed resume moves the session to the SSH channel, with the same messages (tiles latest-wins). A session with no transport after 20 s ends.
  - **Idle timeout:** `Session.Touch` is called on every message A receives (not on tiles sent or keep-alives). The QUIC idle timeout is 15 s with a 5 s keep-alive.
  - **New error tags:** 15 `Transport*`, plus the protocol tags reused for `Missing`, all route-tested.
- **Tests:** all on loopback:
  - a session over QUIC with the real differ, presenter and screen;
  - refused: a wrong or expired token, a wrong certificate, bad Hellos (type, version, magic, a lying length, silence), another ALPN, the pending limit;
  - tiles of every size, stale tiles dropped, lost tiles asked for and resent;
  - the fallback with UDP blocked (a dropping proxy and a fake clock);
  - resume, and resume falling back to SSH;
  - bounded queues and malformed datagrams;
  - the hub end to end with the real 15.2 login, the idle timeout, turning Remote Work off freeing the UDP port;
  - `Missing` round trips and refusals.

  The agent ran the transport and hub tests with `-count=5 -race`; they passed.
- **Lossy-link measurement** (`TestLossyLinkMeasurement`: a UDP proxy with 50 ms each way, the real differ on the 1280x800 fixtures, 4 s of typing at 10 pictures a second). The agent's run:

  | Link | First picture (260 tiles) | Typing: shown / complete per s | Typing latency median / p95 | Scroll (132 tiles, 129 KB) |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | no loss, no delay | 17 ms | 10.0 / 10.0 | 2 / 2 ms | 14 ms |
  | no loss, 50 ms each way | 197 ms | 6.8 / 6.8 | 54 / 58 ms | 278 ms |
  | 5% loss, 50 ms each way | 1.45 s | 6.2 / 5.0 | 55 / 221 ms | 3.6 s |

  Over 6 seeds of the lossy link: first picture 0.78–1.41 s, typing 6.0–7.3 pictures shown a second, scroll 3.4–4.6 s.

  My run: first picture 1.43 s; typing 5.5 shown and 5.0 complete a second, latency median 55 ms and p95 343 ms; scroll 2.6 s, and 3.4 s back; 80 of 1,537 packets dropped and 7 requests for lost tiles.
- **Goldens:** none (no UI in this step).
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · `FuzzDecode` 30 s (`-parallel=1`, `GOMEMLIMIT=2GiB`) ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** none (no UI; the lossy-link measurement above is the step's evidence).
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.18 "As built (step 15.3)" holds the decisions:
  - the datagram fragments and the `Missing` repair;
  - the refresh and the retry live in `Presenter`;
  - a window of 2 pictures, a 400 ms hold, a retry 600 ms after a picture is out, a 5 s refresh;
  - a mismatched certificate is fatal;
  - the SSH Hello is the fallback signal;
  - 3 resume tries.

  No forward error correction. 15.4 feeds the headless view to `Presenter.Present` and the tab to `Screen`.

## 15.4 The remote window tab

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-15.4-remote-tab
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.18, §10.14, §10.15.
  - **Window tab kind `emetos.remotework.tab`** ("Remote Work..." in the `+` menu): a connect form with host, port (47123), and a private key file (Choose...) or an SSH agent key (by comment and fingerprint), with a place for 15.5's QR code.
    - Connect unlocks the key (the passphrase dialog, asked once and kept unlocked only in memory for the tab's life), logs in (15.2's first-use and changed-key dialogs), asks for the workspace when A has several, and opens the 15.3 transport.
    - The tab is titled `<host>: <workspace>` with the remote icon.
    - Cards show connecting, reconnecting (over the last picture), and ended (Connect Again, Edit Connection, Close Tab).
  - **The picture** is the tab's size in logical pixels, drawn from `transport.Screen`. A resize is sent after 150 ms.
  - **Input:** keys with their text, input-method commits, mouse (moves coalesced at 8 ms) and scroll go to A.
    - A new `WindowTab.CaptureKeys` keeps keys away from B's workbench while the tab has focus.
    - B keeps its window and tab shortcuts (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 80).
  - **Clipboard:**
    - A's copy goes to B's clipboard;
    - B's paste chord sends B's clipboard text first, then the key;
    - at most 1 MiB, and never polled.
  - **Notifications:** A forwards its view's notifications (except its own Remote Work ones), with their actions run on A (new `HeadlessView.OnNotification` and `RunNotificationAction`, `notify.Service.RunAction`, `notify.Presenter.Mute`).
  - **Reconnection:** network failures (and A's `ReasonClosed`) retry after 1, 2, 4 ... 30 s. A refused key, Remote Work turned off, an unknown workspace or a changed host key end with their card.
  - **A's side** (`hostview.go`):
    - B's first Resize opens a headless view, and later ones resize it;
    - frames go to `Presenter`, input goes in order on the UI thread, and copies become Clipboard messages;
    - a closed view sends Disconnect (workspace closed), and any unexpected message ends the session with `ReasonProtocol`.
  - **Saved state:** host, port, key choice, key file, fingerprint and workspace, never a passphrase or token. A restored tab shows its form and does not connect by itself.
  - **Dev command:** `dev.remotework.connect` (debug and `emetos_dev` builds only).
  - **`internal/archtest`:** `_test.go` files under `internal/gui/` may import extensions (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 77).
  - **New error tags:** 7, all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - In `extensions/remotework`, A's hub and B's tab in one process on loopback:
    - connect, resize, input, clipboard, notifications;
    - every disconnect reason;
    - reconnection (passphrase asked once), keep trying and give up;
    - a refused key, Remote Work off on the host;
    - first-use and changed host key;
    - the picker, closing the tab, the form, the file chooser, and an agent on a fake socket.
  - In `internal/gui/app`, two whole Emetos apps in one process:
    - the `+` menu entry;
    - B types into A's file, and A's edits show on B;
    - copy and paste both ways;
    - the shortcut split (the palette goes to A, Ctrl+Alt+PageUp stays on B);
    - a notification action runs on A;
    - Disconnect on A, then Connect Again;
    - a dropped connection reconnects;
    - closing the tab ends the session and the view on A.

    Each checks for goroutine leaks.

  The agent ran them with `-race -count=3`; they passed.
- **Goldens:** new `tab_{form,form_agent,connecting,connected,reconnecting,ended_host,ended_refused,picker}_{dark,light}`. I viewed `tab_form_dark`, `tab_reconnecting_light`, `tab_ended_refused_dark` and the proof; the agent viewed all 16. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · display tests none.
  - In the agent's race run, `TestTheCostOfAKeyIsBounded` (editor) failed once under load (8.4 ms against an 8 ms bound; 1.7 ms alone), and passed in mine.
  - I changed it to take the fastest of 5 batches. Re-run with and without `-race`, it reads 1.6 ms.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/15.4-remote-tab.png`, made with:
  ```
  scripts/proof-remotework.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/15.4-remote-tab.png
  ```
  The script, with the helper `scripts/proof-remotework-setup`:
  - makes a fresh client key in a temporary folder that is removed afterwards;
  - writes A's `remote-work.json` with it;
  - starts A and B isolated, each in its own headless compositor and under `timeout -k`;
  - pre-pins A's host key in B's isolated `remote-work-hosts.json` (the first-use dialog is covered by tests and the 15.2 proof);
  - connects B with `dev.remotework.connect`.

  B's window shows its own `local` tab and the tab `127.0.0.1: project` showing A's workspace with `main.go` open.

  ![15.4 remote tab](implemented-proof-images/15.4-remote-tab.png)

## 15.5 Public keys by QR code

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-15.5-key-qr
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.18 "Public keys by QR code", §12.
  - **Dependency:** `github.com/makiuchi-d/gozxing` v0.1.1, which brings in `golang.org/x/xerrors`.
    - Its license is MIT, not Apache-2.0 as the step said; the step's text is corrected.
    - `third_party/gozxing/` holds its MIT license and the Apache-2.0 license of ZXing, which it ports. `NOTICE` entry 15.
  - **`extensions/internal/keyqr`:**
    - **V4L2 through ioctls** with `golang.org/x/sys/unix`, without cgo, for 64-bit amd64 and arm64. Struct sizes, offsets and ioctl numbers are tested against the kernel's.
      - Capture devices only. MJPEG at 1280x720 is preferred, else YUYV at 640x480.
      - Four mmap buffers; the newest frame wins.
      - A poll in 50 ms slices, so a cancel or `Close` is answered quickly.
      - Errnos become tags (busy, denied, gone).
    - **Frames:** MJPEG with the standard Huffman tables inserted when missing, and YUYV to `image.YCbCr`. Dimensions are checked before decoding, and frames over 1920x1080 or 16 MiB are refused.
    - **QR decoding** with gozxing (TRY_HARDER, also at 2/3 and 1/2 scale), off the UI thread, one frame every 100 ms. The text is parsed with the 15.2 authorized-key rules.
    - **QR encoding** of a public line only: level M, else L, up to 452 px with whole pixels per module.
  - **`ui.Picture` and `ui.Image`** (`extension/ui`, `internal/gui/kit/image.go`).
  - **Scan QR Code** on the Remote Work page:
    - the button is disabled, with the reason, when there is no camera or no permission;
    - a live preview with a camera picker;
    - the first valid key stops the camera and shows its type, fingerprint and comment (Cancel, Scan Again, Add Key);
    - other QR codes say "not a public key";
    - no frame is kept.
  - **Show My Public Key as QR Code** in the "Remote Work..." form shows the public half only. It comes from the `.pub`, or from the clear header of an OpenSSH key, or from the agent key; a PEM key asks for its passphrase.
  - **Dev commands:** `dev.keyqr.fakeCamera` and `dev.remotework.scanQR` (debug and `emetos_dev` builds only).
  - **New error tags:** 22, all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - QR round trips for Ed25519, ECDSA and RSA 4096: plain, rotated (5, 15, -30, 45 degrees), blurred, with noise, and all of these at once, at 1280x720 and 640x480. Ed25519 reads 54/54 frames; ECDSA 53/54; RSA 4096 40/54 at 1280x720 and 22/54 at 640x480.
  - Text that is not a key is refused.
  - V4L2 against a fake driver: format choice, YUYV, buffer cycling, newest frame, cancel and close.
  - The dialogs with a fake camera playing frames: they close on the first key, ignore other codes, and release the camera.
  - The image widget.
  - The real-camera test (tag `emetos_camera`) is a manual check.
- **Goldens:**
  - new: 10 in `keyqr/qrui`, 6 in `remotework`, and the image widget's;
  - changed: 14 Remote Work page and tab goldens gain the Scan QR Code button (disabled, with its reason) or the Show My Public Key row;
  - viewed: I viewed `page_off_light`, `tab_form_light` and both proofs; the agent viewed all new ones and 10 of the 14 changed ones.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude` and `emetos_camera`, the latter vet only) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · display tests none. No real camera was opened by any test, proof or check.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/15.5-scan-qr.png` (the confirmation of a scanned key) and `15.5-scan-qr-preview.png` (the live preview with the camera picker), with a fake camera fed a key generated at run time:
  ```
  scripts/proof.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/15.5-scan-qr.png --config testdata/proof/15.5/config --state testdata/proof/15.5/state --commands '[["sleep","1s"],["dev.keyqr.fakeCamera",{"comment":"alice@laptop"}],["emetos.remotework.manage",null],["sleep","1s"],["dev.remotework.scanQR",null],["sleep","4s"]]' --after 3s -- testdata/proof/15.5/project
  ```

## 16.1 Core request topics, privileges and confinement

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-16.1-relay-core
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.19, §10.15.
  - **Core request topics**, answered by the requester's own workspace scope (a window tab scope gets `EventNoHandler`), with payload types in `extension/core.go`:

    | Topic | Notes |
    |---|---|
    | `emetos.commands.list` | Refused commands are marked with `refused`, `refusedClass` and `reason`. |
    | `emetos.commands.execute` | Needs the `commands.execute` privilege. |
    | `emetos.files.list` | Depth at most 16, at most 5000 entries a page, a folder of at most 100,000 entries. |
    | `emetos.files.stat` | |
    | `emetos.files.read` | UTF-8, at most 2 MiB, refused rather than truncated. |
    | `emetos.document.open` | |
    | `emetos.document.read` | At most 8 MiB. |
    | `emetos.document.edit` | One undo step, a stale version refused, at most 10,000 edits and 4 MiB. |
    | `emetos.document.save` | Through the normal save path: participants and format on save. |
    | `emetos.document.close` | A dirty document needs `discard`. |
    | `emetos.editor.reveal` | |

    File requests run off the UI thread in at most 4 slots per scope; documents and commands run on it.
  - **`Manifest.Privileges`** (`events.publishAny`, `commands.execute`) is granted only to `emetos.relay`. Another extension declaring one is not installed and shows under "Left out" on the Extensions page (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 84). The page shows host problems for the first time.
  - **Commands refused to clients** are defined once, in `command.ClientRefusal`, with classes `windowTab`, `quit`, `window`, `folder`, `profile`, `settings` and `remote` (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 86). Each command a client runs is logged.
  - **Confinement** (`internal/core/confine`):
    - `os.Root` with kernel-side resolution beneath the folder (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 85);
    - fifos opened non-blocking and refused;
    - the opened file compared with the one that was checked;
    - document topics checked with `confine.Contains`.
  - **`fakeapi`:** `OnRequest` and `OnRequestValue` doubles, `Requests()`, `PublishedEvents()`, and `Ask`, for 16.2 and 16.3.
  - **New error tags:** about 40 (`ExtensionPrivilege*`, `RelayCommandRefused`, `RelayStaleVersion`, `Core*`, `Confine*`, `ExtDocument*`), all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - `confine`: `..`, absolute paths, links out (relative and absolute), a link loop, a fifo, a folder reached through a link, paging, caps, cancellation, a file swapped while reading.
  - `command`: every refused class with real command ids, plus a scan for risky commands that are neither refused nor allowed.
  - `exthost`:
    - privileges given to Relay and refused to others;
    - `publishAny`;
    - every refused class, and a check that a refused command did not run;
    - the error table;
    - two tabs, so a command run in one does not reach the other;
    - every file topic through requests;
    - the document topics: stale version, bad and overlapping edits, limits, one undo step, read-only, dirty close.
  - `app`: the topics on a real workspace (open, edit, save to disk, reveal, a real core command, a refused one), and the page problem.
- **Goldens:** new `extensions_page_problem_{dark,light}`. I viewed the dark one; the agent viewed both. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude` and `emetos_camera`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · display tests none.
- **Proof:** none (the step has no proof item; the Extensions page golden shows the refused privilege).

  ![16.1 refused privilege](../internal/gui/app/testdata/goldens/extensions_page_problem_dark.png)
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.19 "As built (step 16.1)":
  - `os.Root` instead of `EvalSymlinks` and a check, because it closes the race;
  - an escape is recognized by the text of `os.Root`'s error (if that text ever changes, the path is still refused, under a generic tag);
  - `commands.execute` replies `{result:null}`;
  - `document.read` works on any open document;
  - `emetos.commands` is a reserved extension ID;
  - `NewHeadlessView` stays unprivileged.

## 16.2 The Relay host

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-16.2-relay-host
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.19.
  - **`extensions/relay`** (`emetos.relay`, granted `events.publishAny` and `commands.execute`): the Relay window tab kind ("Relay" in the `+` menu) and its Be a Host section.
    - A listen switch and port (47124).
    - The workspace tabs with a share checkbox each.
    - The allowed keys, added by pasting, by a `.pub` file or by Scan QR Code. Each key has No access, Watch only or Watch and send per shared workspace (Watch only by default).
    - The host fingerprint and the security notes.
  - **`relay.json`** (0600, written only by the tab) and its own host key (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 87). The listener runs only while the switch is on.
  - **Protocol** (`extensions/relay/protocol`, version 1, magic `EMPA`): `type:1 | length:4 | JSON`, with Hello (with the granted workspaces), Subscribe, Event, Publish, Request, Execute, Reply/Result and Disconnect.
    - A per-type limit is checked before reading (1 MiB values, 16 MiB replies).
    - Unknown types and wrong directions are refused.
    - Strict JSON: UTF-8, depth at most 64, unknown fields refused, nothing after the object.
  - **Hub and sessions:**
    - events of each granted workspace are forwarded through a bounded queue per client (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 88);
    - with Watch and send, publish, request and execute run in that workspace's scope only, through the 16.1 topics;
    - Watch only refuses sends (`RelayWatchOnly`);
    - permissions are checked when a message is read and again on the UI thread;
    - at most 16 operations per client and 32 sessions;
    - opaque workspace IDs (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 89);
    - a new Hello when grants change; removing a key or turning hosting off ends its sessions.
  - **Banner** "Relay: <comment> connected (watch only | watch and send)" with Disconnect, notifications (refused logins throttled per address), and logs without event text or arguments.
  - **`extensions/relay/client`** (and `clienttest`, a host double) for 16.3.
  - **Shared code moved out of Remote Work:** `extensions/internal/pageui`, `statefile.DirFromEnv`, `sshlogin.ReadPublicKeyFile`, `HostOf`, `RefusalWords` and `Throttle`. Three Remote Work tags became shared `SSHLogin*`/`StateFile*` tags (SPEC §10.18 updated).
  - **Dev knob:** `EMETOS_DEV_RELAY_BIND` (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 90).
  - **New error tags:** about 40 `Relay*`, plus 3 shared ones, all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - protocol: round trips, every limit, malformed input, the seeded random test, `FuzzDecode`;
  - config modes and read policy;
  - the hub on loopback with real SSH clients:
    - listed and unlisted keys, passwords, the rate limit, one channel type;
    - forwarding to granted workspaces only;
    - Watch only against Watch and send;
    - core error tags reaching the client;
    - permission changes and unsharing mid-session;
    - key removal and hosting off;
    - banner and Disconnect;
    - several clients;
    - protocol violations, operation limits, drop counting, the stall watchdog.
  - the tab's controls and Scan QR Code;
  - the client package;
  - the privilege registration through `extensions.All()`;
  - 4 end-to-end tests over real windows in `internal/gui/app`.

  The agent ran the end-to-end, relay, protocol and client tests with `-race -count=3`; they passed.
- **Goldens:** new `tab_{on,off,port_taken}_{dark,light}`. I viewed `tab_port_taken_light` and the proof; the agent viewed all six. No existing golden changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude` and `emetos_camera`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · `FuzzDecode` 30 s (`-parallel=1`, `GOMEMLIMIT=2GiB`) ✅ · display tests none.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/16.2-relay-host.png`, made with:
  ```
  scripts/proof-relay.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/16.2-relay-host.png
  ```
  The script fills `testdata/proof/16.2/state/relay.json.template` (public keys only) with the checkout path and a free port, and listens on 127.0.0.1 only.

  The tab shows hosting on and two shared workspaces, `notes` and `server`:
  - alice@laptop has Watch only in notes and Watch and send in server;
  - bob@desktop has Watch only in notes and No access to server.

  The script prints a failed `--launch` at the end, which also happens with the existing 9b.3 proof; the image is complete.

  ![16.2 relay host](implemented-proof-images/16.2-relay-host.png)
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.19 "As built (step 16.2)" lists the decisions:
  - only an open workspace with a folder can be shared, and two tabs of one folder share the first;
  - a listen failure is shown, and the switch stays on;
  - the request timeout is 10 s by default and 60 s at most.

  16.3 builds the Hosts section, the Events and Send panels, and `relay-hosts.json`.

## 16.3 The Relay client

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-16.3-relay-client
- **Summary:** SPEC §10.19 "The tab".
  - **Hosts section** of the Relay tab:
    - **Add Host:** address, port 47124, an optional name, a key file or the SSH agent, and Show My Public Key as QR Code.
    - **Saved hosts:** each shows its state (disconnected, connecting, connected with its permission, connection lost with the attempt, ended with a reason), with Connect, Disconnect and Remove. A connected host lists its workspaces.
    - **Several hosts at once**, one goroutine each.
    - **Host key pinning** in `relay-hosts.json`: first use asks, and a changed key is refused, with "Forget the Old Key".
    - **Reconnection:** 1 s doubling to 30 s after a drop. Host decisions and refused keys are not retried. An unlocked key file is kept in memory only, until Disconnect.
  - **Events panel:**
    - a virtualized reverse `ListView`, with JSON built only when a row is expanded;
    - batches with their count, and the host's dropped markers;
    - this client's requests with reply or error and duration;
    - a filter (text or `*` pattern), Pause (holds rows) and Clear;
    - a cap of 10,000 rows, 16 MiB, and 4 KiB per value;
    - events reach the UI thread in drains (one timer and one `Post`).
  - **Send panel** (hidden for Watch only):
    - a topic and JSON value with Publish and Request;
    - Run Command, with the host's commands from `emetos.commands.list` (refused ones dimmed) and JSON arguments;
    - the reply or the error with its tag;
    - a history of 50 items, none over 8 KiB, kept in the tab state.
  - **Saved state:** hosts, selection (host and workspace name) and history; never a passphrase (tested).
  - **Shared code:** `extensions/internal/loginui` (moved from Remote Work, with Relay's wording) and `extensions/internal/keyform` (the key choice of both connect forms; Remote Work's goldens are pixel-identical).
  - **Fix in the 16.2 host:** a lock-order deadlock (`Hub.forward` held the hub lock while asking sessions; `Session.applyGrants` held a session lock while asking the hub).
    - Found when this step's race run hung; the goroutine dump showed the cycle.
    - Both sides now copy what they need and release the first lock before taking the second, and the lock order is documented on `Hub`.
    - Regression test `TestForwardingAndGrantChangesRunTogetherWithoutDeadlock`: it hung on the old code and passes with `-race -count=20`.
  - **Fix in the 16.2 tab:** it disposed its input boxes off the UI thread on close (a data race); it now does so in `OnClose`.
  - **New error tags:** 7, all route-tested.
- **Tests:**
  - **Tab** (fake API, real hosts on loopback behind a proxy that can drop the link):
    - first use, a refused first use, a changed key and Forget;
    - events, filter, Pause, Clear, the 10,000 cap, expanding a row, a burst of 5,000 events as one drain, the dropped marker;
    - Send, Run Command, the history bound;
    - Watch only has no Send panel;
    - reconnection, the passphrase asked once and never saved, the agent, several hosts, the end reasons, Remove;
    - closing the tab with a goroutine leak check;
    - state save and restore.
  - **End to end** with two real windows (`internal/gui/app/relayclient_test.go`):
    - `emetos.document.changed` arrives while the host edits;
    - `files.list`, document open, read and edit, a stale version refused, save;
    - an extension's command and topic;
    - refusals outside the workspace;
    - Watch only;
    - reconnection, two hosts, pinning.

  The agent ran the new tests and touched packages with `-race -count=3` before its later fixes, and `extensions/relay` with `-race -count=10` after them; they passed.
- **Goldens:**
  - new: `client_{events,send,watchonly,hosts}_{dark,light}`;
  - changed: the six 16.2 `tab_*`, which gain the Hosts section above Be a Host;
  - viewed: I viewed `client_events_light` and the proof; the agent viewed all.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_claude` and `emetos_camera`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `emetos_gopls` tests ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · the deadlock test `-race -count=10` ✅ · `FuzzDecode` (Relay protocol) 30 s ✅ · display tests none.
  - The agent's first two full race runs failed: the archtest import rule and the tab-close race, then the deadlock. Both were fixed.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/16.3-relay-client.png`, made with:
  ```
  scripts/proof-relay-client.sh docs/implemented-proof-images/16.3-relay-client.png
  ```
  Two isolated instances on loopback. The host listens on 127.0.0.1 only. Keys are made at run time in a temp folder that is removed, and the host key is pre-pinned in the client's isolated `relay-hosts.json` (tests cover the first-use dialog).

  The client's Relay tab shows:
  - "Office laptop" connected, with `notes` (watch only) and `server` (watch and send);
  - the Events panel with the host's `emetos.document.changed` and the client's requests;
  - the Send panel with the `emetos.files.list` reply.

  ![16.3 relay client](implemented-proof-images/16.3-relay-client.png)
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** new in `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`: a reverse `ListView` does not keep the reader's place when items are added at its start. The workaround is `keepPlace` in `extensions/relay/clienttab.go`.
- **Deviations and follow-ups:** SPEC §10.19 "As built (step 16.3)" (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 91–93). Also:
  - the same `host:port` twice is refused, and a tab holds at most 64 hosts;
  - selecting another workspace starts a new log;
  - a refused command is not sent by the client.

## Fix: Glutter 1.9.1

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** fix/glutter-1.9.1 (not a step of the plan)
- **Summary:** Emetos moves to Glutter v1.9.1. That fixes the 15.1 entry of `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`, "No public way to draw a view off screen and to read its pixels".
  - **The headless view runs on `offscreen.View`.**
    - The hand-made binding, the `PlatformHost`, the `gluttertest.Tester` used as a rasterizer and `headlessPaceFactor` are gone (`headless_engine.go` is deleted).
    - Frames are paced by `MaxFrameRate` (30), and are drawn on the view's goroutine, redrawing only the damage.
    - `gluttertest` is no longer in the editor binary.
    - The extension API is unchanged.
  - **The view's clipboard:** the view keeps a clipboard of its own, through a parent binding that never draws (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 94). Without it, Glutter's view would have reached the host's desktop clipboard.
  - **Numbers at 1280x800:**
    - a typed character takes 4.7 ms of the UI thread (82 ms with 1.8.3) and reaches `OnFrame` about 20 ms after the key;
    - the view takes at most about 14% of the UI thread;
    - the binary is 88.3 MB with the release tag (88.6 MB before).
  - **The tile differ** does not use the damage yet (DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW 95).
  - **The view's caret** stays solid.
- **Main files:** `go.mod`, `internal/gui/app/headless.go`, `headless_clipboard.go` (new), `headless_engine.go`, `race_test.go` and `norace_test.go` (deleted), `docs/SPEC.md` (§10.18 "As built (Glutter 1.9.1)"), `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`.
- **Tests:**
  - **clipboard:** the find and quick input boxes of a view never read or write the host's clipboard or primary selection; the paste is the client's text and copies reach `OnCopy`. Removing the fix makes these tests fail with the host's sentinel text.

## Step 17b.1: Real input, and typing reaches every input box

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.1-real-input
- **Summary:** Emetos is now tested with real keyboard and pointer events (a Wayland virtual keyboard and pointer in sway). Every input box keeps taking keys after the first one, which fixes the desktop bug "the keyboard stopped working until I clicked again the input box".
- **Cause:** `kit.InputBox` removed the placeholder's `Stack` at the first key. That mounted the `EditableText` again with the focus already on its node, and Glutter opens the input connection only on a focus change, so every later key was dropped.
- **Fix:**
  - the input box's tree is the same with or without the placeholder;
  - a click in a focused box with no connection reconnects it (`RequestKeyboard`);
  - the quick input takes typed keys from the moment it is shown, and a focus node takes the arrows, Enter and Escape at once;
  - `kit.TypeAhead` (new) does the same for Find, Replace, the rename box and the explorer's inline input;
  - the editor's `Stack` layers are keyed, so a layer that appears no longer remounts the rename box.
- **Also found by real input and fixed:**
  - Ctrl+A, Ctrl+Z, Ctrl+Y and Ctrl+Shift+Z in an input box went to the editor's commands; the box now runs them on its own text;
  - a group's focus retry could take the focus from a quick input opened just after it and close it.
- **Harness:**
  - `internal/testenv/realinput`: the Wayland client, with one keyboard and one pointer for the whole run, plus sway IPC;
  - `internal/testenv/display`: starts an isolated debug Emetos, sends real input, reads state through the dev socket, takes screenshots;
  - dev server `EMETOS_DEV_SOCKET`, with the read-only `dev.inputs` and `dev.editor` (SPEC §15.2);
  - `scripts/realinput.sh [--desktop]`: runs under the headless sway, or in a nested sway window on the desktop.
- **Main files:** `internal/gui/kit/inputbox.go`, `kit/typeahead.go` (new), `internal/gui/quickinput/host.go`, `internal/gui/editor/{widget,findstate,rename_widget}.go`, `internal/gui/views/explorer/{edit,view}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{keys,group,devserver,devquery}.go`, `internal/testenv/`, `cmd/emetos/realinput_display_test.go`, `scripts/realinput.sh`, `docs/SPEC.md` (§10.2, §10.6, §15.1, §15.2 "As built (step 17b.1)").
- **Tests:**
  - 12 real-input display tests: Quick Open, Find, settings search, keybindings search, explorer rename, Notes, Problems filter, scratchpad, edit shortcuts, command palette, Go to Line, and the proofs. Before the fix, ten of them failed (`app.go` gave `"p"`, `hello world` gave `"h"`); the command palette and Go to Line tests passed and are kept as regression tests;
  - unit tests through the platform's key path: `TestInputBoxTypedKeysReachTheBox`, the click reconnect, the edit shortcuts, `TestHostTakesTheKeysTypedBeforeItsFirstFrame` and `TestEditChordsInATextFieldAreTheFields` all failed before the fix;
  - `TypeAhead`, dev server and `realinput` tests, with route tests for every new tag;
  - `TestTheInputBoxesOfTheWindowKeepTheClipboardOfTheDesktop` changed: Ctrl+A then Ctrl+V in the command palette now replaces the `>` too, as in VS Code.
- **Goldens:** `keybindings_recording_{dark,light}`, `dialog_passphrase_dark` (asterisks, and the caret in the right place) and agents `completion_commands`; viewed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also with `emetos_display`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ (`internal/gui/app` was killed once for lack of memory while other programs filled the swap, and passed when run alone) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `scripts/realinput.sh` (headless sway, race, 12 tests) ✅.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/17b.1-quickopen.png` (Quick Open with `app.go` typed by the virtual keyboard) and `17b.1-note.png` (a note whose title and two-line body were typed the same way), made with `scripts/realinput.sh --shots docs/implemented-proof-images`.

  ![Quick Open](implemented-proof-images/17b.1-quickopen.png)
  ![Note](implemented-proof-images/17b.1-note.png)
- **Manual checks:** none; the manager's desktop pass is step 17b.5.
- **Glutter issues:** new:
  - an `EditableText` mounted again with the focus on its node gets no input connection;
  - the caret of an obscured field is placed by byte offsets into the bullet string.

## Step 17b.2: A fast Markdown preview

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.2-markdown-preview
- **Summary:** The preview of a large document opens at once and stays light. The preview of a document of 700 KB with 150 images (the size of this file) used to freeze the window and take 2 GB.
- **Changes:**
  - **Lazy list:** the page preview (`markdown.NewView`) builds only the blocks within 250 px of the viewport. Their heights are estimated, then measured, in a Fenwick tree, and positions are anchored to the block at the top, so an image that loads above the viewport moves nothing on screen. `ScrollToLine`, `TopLine` and the editor's scroll sync work as before. Hover, Flow and Compact keep the column.
  - **Images:** `markdown.ImageCache` decodes off the UI thread, two at a time, at the size shown on screen. Its budget is 64 MB, with least recently used images dropped first. Failures are cached and keep the alt text.
  - **Updates:** a change is parsed after a 300 ms throttle (as VS Code's `preview.ts`). A text over 16 KB is parsed off the UI thread. Blocks are keyed by their content, so an edit builds only its block.
  - **Highlighting:** done once per block, from a cache of the last 256 code blocks.
  - **Start:** a restored session with the preview of a large file draws its first frame without waiting for the parse.
  - **Dev command:** `dev.markdown` returns the preview's statistics.
- **Also fixed:** the README's link to SPEC §10.16, which the M17c plan had broken.
- **Main files:** `extension/ui/markdown/{preview,controller,heights,estimate,keys,images,imageview,tokens,render,view}.go`, `extensions/markdown/markdown.go`, `extension/ui/uitest/mdfixture/` (the generated fixture), `internal/testenv/display/framestats.go`, `internal/gui/app/devquery.go`, `cmd/emetos/preview_display_test.go`, `scripts/perf.sh`, `docs/SPEC.md` (§10.15 "As built (step 17b.2)", §14, §15.2), `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`.
- **Tests:**
  - only the visible blocks are built;
  - the wheel and the scroll bar, and `ScrollToLine`/`TopLine` over estimated and measured heights;
  - scrolling before the parse ends;
  - an edit rebuilds only its block, after the delay, and the view stays at its block;
  - the image cache: sizes, budget, eviction, failures, abandoned decodes, with route tests for its tags;
  - `BenchmarkPreview*`;
  - real-input display tests: `TestRealInputBigPreviewOpensAndScrolls`, its proof, and `...StartDoesNotWaitForThePreview`;
  - before the fix, the display scenario took 9.2 s to answer, used 2,096 MB more, and drew every frame in over 16 ms.
- **Numbers** (headless, llvmpipe, 1280x800):

  | Metric | Before | After |
  |---|---|---|
  | Open to first blocks | 9.1–9.6 s | 157–259 ms |
  | Memory over the file as text | +2,090–2,290 MB | +116–129 MB |
  | Blocks built | 3,224 | 7–11 |
  | Raster per frame, average | 786 ms | 12.6–18 ms |

  - The worst UI-thread frame, 40–69 ms, is the first frame after a jump, which highlights code on the UI thread.
  - The GPU numbers come from the desktop pass, step 17b.5.
- **Goldens:** none changed; `preview_dark.png` viewed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ (my full run: 100 packages, exit 0) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `scripts/realinput.sh` ✅.
  - `TestAPictureThatWaitedForOnFrameAndARefreshAreHandedOver` (`internal/gui/app`) failed in the agent's run while the machine was short of memory, also on main. It passed 3 times out of 3 when run again; it is timing-sensitive under load.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/17b.2-preview.png`, made through the real-input harness: the preview of the fixture scrolled to its middle.

  ![Preview](implemented-proof-images/17b.2-preview.png)
- **Manual checks:** none; the GPU numbers are part of step 17b.5.
- **Glutter issues:** new:
  - `SliverList` lays out every item between its first one and the offset of a jump;
  - the Vulkan renderer draws shapes outside the clip (the 17b.4 item; the preview keeps a small cache margin because of it).

## Step 17b.3: Brackets, snippets and signature help as in VS Code

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.3-overtype-snippets
- **Summary:** Typing `)` before a `)` that the user typed inserts a new one, as VS Code does. The closing `)` of a completion is typed over and no longer stays in a growing tab stop. The signature help closes when its answer is stale.
- **Causes and fixes:**
  - **Typing over the user's `)`**
    - *Cause:* the input method hands the editor the whole line, and the change was worked out from the first and last differing characters. `// z)` → `// z))` read as "a `)` replaced by itself", so it took the composition path, which skips typing-over and auto-closing.
    - *Fix:* `editedStretch` (`internal/gui/editor/input.go`) starts from the input method's selection, as VS Code's `TextAreaState.deduceInput` does.
  - **The closing `)` of a completion**
    - *Cause:* gopls sends `Println(${1:})`. VS Code tracks the closing character at the end of a snippet as auto-closed (`cursor.ts` `_findAutoClosingPairs`); Emetos did not, so a typed `)` went into the empty `$1`, which grew.
    - *Fix:* ported as `edit.SnippetPairs` / `SnippetAutoClosed`, through a new `snippet.Editor.OnAutoClosed` hook (completions, `insertSnippet` and Emmet).
  - **Signature help**
    - *Cause:* an answer about changed text was dropped, but the request stayed pending, so the old hints stayed on screen.
    - *Fix:* it now cancels the request. Closing on cursor moves and on Escape already matched VS Code.
  - **A snippet with only `$0`** (`Sprintf($0)`) already ended its session at once. Regression tests were added; they passed before the fix.
- **Main files:** `internal/gui/editor/{input,snippets,signature,dev}.go`, `internal/core/edit/snippetclosed.go` (new), `internal/core/snippet/session.go`, `internal/core/autoclose/autoclose.go`, `internal/core/lsp/lsptest/` (an `insideParens` answer, as gopls does for signature help), `internal/gui/app/devquery.go` (`dev.editor` gives `inSnippet`, `tabStops`, `suggestVisible` and `signatureVisible`), `cmd/emetos/editing_display_test.go` (new), `docs/SPEC.md` (§8.6.4, §9.9.2, §9.12, §15.1 "As built (step 17b.3)").
- **Tests:**
  - `TestSnippetPairs` (17 cases) and `TestSnippetAutoClosed`, from `_findAutoClosingPairs`;
  - `TestEditedStretch` (15) and `TestKeysTypeOver...` (6; 3 failed before the fix);
  - snippet, controller, signature, Emmet and `dev.editor` tests;
  - VS Code's 10 `autoClosingOvertype` cases, already ported, still pass.
  - **Real input**, with a fake language server, and how each did on the unfixed code:
    - `TestRealInputTypingOverAClosingBracketTheUserTyped` failed, giving `// z)`;
    - `TestRealInputCompletionWithAFinalTabStopEndsTheSnippet` (Enter and Tab) failed, giving `fmt.Sprintf(("x")`, with the signature help still open;
    - `TestRealInputTypingOverTheClosingBracketOfACompletion` failed with the snippet fix removed, giving `fmt.Println("x"))`;
    - `TestRealInputSignatureHelpClosesWhenTheCursorLeavesTheArguments` passed before the fix; it is a guard.
- **Goldens:** none changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ (100 packages) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `scripts/realinput.sh` ✅ (19 tests).
- **Proof:** the real-input tests above, which type with a virtual keyboard and read the document through `dev.editor`; the step asks for no image.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none

## Step 17b.4: The Glutter issues and the cost of an idle window

- **Date:** 2026-10-27
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.4-glutter-idle
- **Summary:** Glutter's bugs from the desktop test are written down with the fix Glutter should make. The crash of the gesture arena is avoided in Emetos, and Emetos's own share of what an idle window allocates is almost halved. What is left is Glutter's renderer.
- **Gesture arena (crash):**
  - *What triggers it:* every tap target is the lone member of an arena that a mouse moving away rejects. Slow frames batch the pointer events, and Linux gives the mouse pointer id 0, so the posted `resolveByDefault` takes a member of an old, empty arena and panics.
  - *Workaround:* `fixGestureArena` (`internal/gui/app/arenafix.go`, `// Glutter:`) gives the binding an arena manager whose microtasks run before the next pointer event, as Flutter's do. It is installed at the window root and in each headless view.
- **Idle cost:**
  - **Emetos's share:** the cursors layer read the text of every visible row on every blink to find control characters. `controlCache` (`internal/gui/editor/display_paint.go`) now keeps them per row until the document changes.
  - **Glutter's share:** 99% of what an idle frame allocates is Glutter's renderer, which flattens the whole scene every frame (new `GLUTTER-ISSUES.md` entry). It is garbage, not a leak: the live heap stays flat.

  | Case | Before | After |
  |---|---|---|
  | Emetos's share, per blink frame, 10,000-line Go file (headless) | 17.0 KB, 283 allocations | 10.5 KB, 132 allocations |
  | Same, with the Markdown preview | 24.3 KB | 13.1 KB |
  | Whole frame, Go file | 1.94 MB | 1.87 MB |
  | Whole frame, preview | 8.4 MB | 8.5 MB |
- **Main files:** `internal/gui/app/arenafix.go` (new), `tray.go`, `headless.go`, `internal/gui/editor/display_paint.go`, `overlay.go`, `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`, `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` ("Idle allocation"), `docs/SPEC.md` (§14 "As built (step 17b.4)").
- **Tests:**
  - `TestGestureArenaSurvivesPointerIDReuseInOneTurn` (Glutter alone panics with `index out of range [0] with length 0`);
  - `TestTheArenaIsFixedInTheApplicationAndInTheView` (fails if either call is removed);
  - `controlcache_test.go` (an unchanged frame allocates nothing);
  - `TestIdleBlinkFrameAllocationStaysInBudget`: 10,000-line Go file, budget 6 KB and 120 allocations per blink frame. It measured 8.0 KB and 147 allocations before the fix, 4.6 KB and 92 after.
- **Goldens:** none changed.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display`) · golangci-lint ✅ · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ · `scripts/realinput.sh` ✅ (my full run, exit 0; the agent's two runs had timing failures while the machine was at load 50, each passing alone).
- **Proof:** the tests above; the step asks for no image.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:**
  - completed: the renderer draws shapes outside the clip and hashes every path every frame;
  - new:
    - the gesture arena resolves on the next loop turn, and the mouse always has pointer id 0;
    - the keyboard logs `no_keymap` at error level;
    - the renderers flatten the whole scene every frame.

## Change: the hairball icon, redrawn

- **Date:** 2026-10-28

- **Summary:** The icon was redrawn after the user said the first hairball (a brown, lumpy sausage with a dark outline in a puddle) "looks like a poop", then in orange and white colors, and finally as a round ball: the user asked "Make it a ball!! Round ball! Like basketball!!". The hairball is now a round ball of 96 curved hair strokes in six colors from deep orange to white that wrap around a sphere like yarn on a ball of wool (pale where the light falls from the top left, deep orange at the bottom right), a small damp highlight and a fuzzy edge of short hairs with four longer strays, over a radial gradient disc; no outline, no puddle, no brown. ## Step 17b.5: A desktop pass

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.5-desktop-pass
- **Summary:** Emetos was used with real keyboard and mouse input (a Wayland virtual keyboard and pointer in sway) through everything it offers. Three bugs were fixed. Five more findings are planned in step 17b.6, and the manager's pass on the user's desktop moved to the end of that step. Every finding is in `docs/DESKTOP-PASS.md`.
- **Fixed:**
  - **D1, a click in the explorer moved the focus to the editor,** so F2, Delete and typed letters went to the file. A click now opens the file without taking the focus (`PreserveFocus`, as VS Code); Enter and a double click take it.
  - **D2, Ctrl+Z after Move to Trash did not undo the delete,** because the focus went to the next editor. The tree takes the focus back.
  - **D3, typing after a save joined the undo step from before the save,** so one Undo went past the saved text. A save now closes the undo group, as VS Code's `doSave` does.
- **Planned (17b.6):**
  - D4: the panel's tab names are cut under the view's actions;
  - D5: keys 10 ms apart race the Find box, and a letter right after F2 opens the tree's find box;
  - D6: the hover stays beside a context menu;
  - D10: the empty Notes view has no New Note button;
  - D11: a window tab's label is cut with room to spare.
- **Not bugs:**
  - D7: there is no Search view yet (SPEC §3 area 4, later);
  - D8: Open Folder needs the session bus, which the test has not;
  - D9: the preview does not scroll its source (SPEC).
- **Main files:**
  - `cmd/emetos/desktop_*_display_test.go` (new: explorer, editing, navigation, settings, diff and merge, layout, extensions, session, and real gopls under `emetos_gopls`);
  - `internal/testenv/display/app.go` (find and click text on screen, a second launch);
  - `internal/gui/app/devworkbench.go` (new `dev.texts` and `dev.workbench`);
  - `internal/gui/app/{group,editors,gridview,explorer,fileops}.go`, `internal/gui/views/explorer/view.go`, `internal/core/document/save.go`;
  - `scripts/realinput.sh` (it now runs `TestRealInput|TestDesktop`);
  - `docs/DESKTOP-PASS.md` (new);
  - `docs/SPEC.md` (§7.1, §10.5, §15.1, §15.2).
- **Tests:**

  - `TestDesktopExplorer/delete_to_the_trash_and_undo`, before the fix: the file did not come back;

  - the other `TestDesktop*` tests cover the areas that passed;

## Fix: Glutter 1.9.2

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** fix/glutter-1.9.2 (not a step of the plan)
- **Summary:** Emetos moves to Glutter v1.9.2, a release made for it (its steps 29.8 to 29.21). All eleven entries of `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md` are fixed, so every `// Glutter:` workaround is removed and the file is deleted. No new indirect dependency came in (`go.mod` changes the Glutter version only).
  - **The Events panel keeps what the user reads (29.8):** `extensions/relay` drops `keepPlace`; the log's `ListView` has `MaintainVisibleContentPosition`, a key on each row (its number in the log) and `FindChildIndexCallback` (`eventLog.indexFromNewest`); `pushedShown` is gone.
  - **The headless view (29.9, 29.10):** `offscreen.ViewOpts.Clipboard`, `PlatformConfiguration` and `Window` replace `clipboardParent`/`clipboardHost`, the `viewSetup` root, `configure` and `applyWindow`; the view's parent is the application's binding.
  - **The gesture arena (29.11, 29.12, 29.17):** `fixGestureArena` (`arenafix.go`) is deleted and no longer installed by the tray host.
  - **Input boxes (29.18, 29.19):** the click handler that reconnected the input method and the `*` obscuring character are gone; passphrase boxes show bullets again, with the caret after the right one.
  - **The Markdown preview (29.13, 29.20):** its own list is kept (decision: Glutter's `EstimateItemOffsets` makes a rewrite on `SliverList` possible, but it is not needed, and the anchor-based list never moves what is on screen); only the `// Glutter:` comments are replaced by plain ones. `cacheMargin` stays at Flutter's 250 px (a jump or a wheel step does not need more).
  - **Not Emetos's code (29.14, 29.15, 29.16):** the "no keymap" log, the flatten of the whole scene on each frame and `testing` in the binary are fixed in Glutter (`go list -deps ./cmd/emetos | grep -x testing` prints nothing).
- **Numbers** (`docs/PERFORMANCE.md`, "After Glutter 1.9.2", headless): an idle blink frame on the UI thread, renderer included, 164 KB and 2,100 allocations with the 10,000-line Go file (1.87 MB and 7,200 before), 549 KB and 5,900 with the Markdown preview (8.54 MB and 30,600 before). Emetos's own part is unchanged (about 11 KB, 190 allocations). The preview: first blocks 141 ms, +113 to +119 MB resident, `BenchmarkPreviewJump` 1.0 ms, UI thread 0.86 ms a frame while scrolling.
- **Main files:** `go.mod`, `go.sum`, `extensions/relay/{eventsview,eventlog,clienttab}.go`, `internal/gui/app/{headless,headless_clipboard,tray}.go`, `internal/gui/kit/inputbox.go`, `extension/ui/markdown/preview.go`, `docs/SPEC.md` (§10.18, §10.19, §10.x preview, §14), `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`, `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md` (deleted).

## Step 17b.6: The findings of the desktop pass

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17b.6-desktop-findings (rebased onto the Glutter 1.9.2 fix)
- **Summary:** the findings that 17b.5 planned (D4, D5, D6, D10, D11) and the two flaky tests (D12) are fixed, each with a regression test that failed before its fix. Every finding of `docs/DESKTOP-PASS.md` is now fixed or not a bug. The manager's pass on the user's desktop is below.
- **Fixed:**
  - **D5, keys typed fast.** The cause was a key that arrived before its input was built and was handled by the old widgets.
    - `kit.TypeAhead` put the keys it took into the input but never told the input's owner, so the find widget did not search.
    - A handler of the hardware keyboard cannot keep the focus tree from seeing a key. So the letter after F2 also started the tree's find, and Enter made a new line.
    - After Ctrl+, no node had the focus for a few frames.
    - The fix: `TypeAhead` takes the key with a hardware handler and claims it from the focus tree with an early handler. It tells the owner through `onChange`, and keeps Enter, the arrows, Home, End and Delete, in order, until the input has the focus. `StartTypeAheadLater` waits for an input that is not built yet (the settings and keybindings editors' search).
  - **D4, the panel's tabs** (`workbench/paneloverflow.go`). The tabs get their room first. The view's actions get what is left and hide under 130 px. Tabs that do not fit go into a `...` menu, and the active tab always shows. A tab is never cut.
  - **D6:** a right click hides the editor's hover (`secondaryDown` calls `HideHover`).
  - **D10:** the empty Notes view shows "No notes yet." over a "New Note" button.
  - **D11:** a window tab is 1 px wider than its measured label (`tabLabelSlack`), so a label is never cut when there is room. `dev.texts` reports `cut` paragraphs.
  - **D12, two flaky tests, both wrong in the test:**
    - `TestAPictureThatWaitedForOnFrameAndARefreshAreHandedOver` read pictures queued before `Close`. It also showed a real window: `headlessView.Close` now waits up to 250 ms for a call of the frame function that is running.
    - `TestTheCoreTopicsDriveARealWorkspace` read the tab strip before the frames that build it.
    - Each now passes 200 runs in a row.
- **Harness:** `realinput.NoKeyDelay`, `TypeBatch`, `PressBatch`, `Hold` and `TextCombos`. `display.App` gets `SetKeyRepeat` (sway's repeat rate) and `ResizeWindow`. The 25 ms delays of the older desktop tests are gone; they type at 10 ms.
- **Main files:**
  - `internal/gui/kit/{typeahead,tabs}.go`, `internal/gui/editor/{findstate,rename_widget,pointer}.go`, `internal/gui/views/explorer/edit.go`;
  - `internal/gui/{settingsui,keybindingsui}/editor.go`, `internal/gui/app/{settingspane,keybindingspane,devworkbench,headless}.go`;
  - `internal/gui/workbench/{paneloverflow,workbench}.go`, `extensions/notes/notes.go`, `extension/headless.go`;
  - `internal/testenv/{display/app.go,realinput/*}`;
  - `docs/DESKTOP-PASS.md`, `docs/SPEC.md` (§15.1 "Real input as built (step 17b.6)"), `docs/GLUTTER-ISSUES.md`.
- **Tests:**
  - `TestDesktopFastKeys{Find,Rename,NewFile,QuickOpen,CommandPalette,SettingsSearch,KeybindingsSearch,Notes}` each run at 10 ms, with no delay, and in one batch. Before the fix:
    - Find: "timed out waiting for the cursor on the end of the first match";
    - Rename: the input was left with "f";
    - Settings search: `last state: ""`.
  - `TestDesktopFastKeysHeld` holds a key at 25 and 50 repeats a second, in the editor and in the find box.
  - `TestFastKeysAfterCtrlFSearch`, `TestKeysTypedRightAfterTheKeyThatOpensTheInputGoToIt` and three `TypeAhead` tests fail when the claim is removed.
  - `TestDesktopPanelTabsAreWholeAndOverflow` checks the tabs at 1280, 1024 and 760 px. `TestPanelTabsAreWholeAndOverflowIntoAMenu` and `TestPanelOverflowMenuListsTheHiddenTabs` also cover D4.
  - `TestDesktopHoverHidesWhenTheContextMenuOpens`, `TestDesktopEmptyNotesViewHasAWelcomeButton`, `TestDesktopWindowTabLabelIsNotCutWhenThereIsRoom`.
  - `TestCloseWaitsForTheCallOfOnFrameThatIsRunning` and `TestAFunctionOfOnFrameMayCloseTheView`.
- **Goldens:** 66 changed in `internal/gui/app` and `internal/gui/workbench`. The window tab is 1 px wider, and only the title bar's tab strip moves. The agent looked at every one, old beside new.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display` and `emetos_gopls`) · golangci-lint ✅ (also `--build-tags emetos_display`) · error-tags --untested ✅ · go test -race ✅ (my run on the final code, 100 packages, exit 0) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ (my run, 100 packages, exit 0) · `scripts/realinput.sh` ✅ (my headless run after the rebase onto Glutter 1.9.2: 101 tests and subtests, exit 0; the D13 agent's run after its change: exit 0).
- **Proof:** the 83 screenshots of the agent's last run, viewed by the agent; I viewed the panel tabs at 1024 px and with the menu, and the Notes welcome.
- **The manager's desktop pass** (`scripts/realinput.sh --desktop` in a nested sway on the user's desktop, Radeon 8060S, Vulkan, `EMETOS_REALINPUT_RACE=0`). I viewed all 83 screenshots and found nothing new in them. Every test passed except `TestRealInputBigPreviewOpensAndScrolls` (4 of 4 runs), which is finding **D13**:
  - the test counted the editor window's first draw as scrolling, because frame statistics are logged once a second and the first window had not been flushed;
  - that first draw is 0.9 s on the GPU and 4 to 5 s on llvmpipe with an empty Mesa shader cache. Every display test starts with one, because `XDG_CACHE_HOME` is fresh. With a warm cache it is 0.29 s;
  - the 100 to 120 ms of UI thread is the debug build's `AssertCurrent` (`runtime.Stack`); the release build takes 10 to 20 ms;
  - the fix: the scrolling is measured from the next stats window (`App.WaitFrameWindow`), and the open's frames have their own budget (`previewFirstFrameBudget`, `App.SlowFrames`);
  - afterwards it passed 2 of 2 on the desktop: 43 to 45 ms for the open's slowest frame (debug build), 0 frames over 16 ms while scrolling, 2.0 to 2.2 ms of renderer per frame.
  - **GPU numbers** (`scripts/perf.sh --session --no-go --no-huge`, `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` "Desktop pass (step 17b.6)"):
    - met: idle with the focus 0.47% of a core, without it 0 frames; typing 2.3 ms a frame; the start with no folder 260 ms;
    - missed: the 10 MB open 410 ms (target 300), the restored start 570 ms (target 400), the worst scrolling build 8.24 ms (target 8).
    - `docs/MANUAL-CHECKS.md` 3.11/10.1 now leaves only the 200 MB open, the Go benchmarks and the decision on the missed targets to the user.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** two new entries. The first: still present in 1.9.2 (its keyboard and focus code is unchanged from 1.9.1). A handler of the hardware keyboard cannot keep the focus tree from seeing a key, and early key handlers do not run while no node has the focus. The workaround is in `kit/typeahead.go`.
  The second (D13): in a debug build, `UIThread.AssertCurrent` calls `runtime.Stack` on every mark-dirty call; there is no workaround in Emetos.

## Step 17c.1: Remove the Agents extension

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17c.1-remove-agents
- **Summary:** the Agents extension is gone, at the user's request of 2026-10-27 (D26): Emetos neither runs nor downloads the Claude Code program, and nothing in it leads the user to third-party terms. Its pseudo-terminal code lives on as `extensions/internal/pty` for the terminal (M17d). A window restored from an older state drops the extension's tabs, with one info log line and no dialog.
- **Removed:**
  - `extensions/agents/` with `claude/`, `claudetest`, `fakeclaude`, its testdata and the `emetos_claude` build tag; its line in `extensions/all.go`; the fixtures `testdata/proof/13.2`, `testdata/proof/13.3` and `testdata/screenshots/agents`; `docs/screenshots/agents.png` and the README's Agents section.
  - With the extension went its settings (`agents.claude.path`, `agents.attachActiveFile`), its commands (`agents.*`, Alt+K, "Add Selection to Agent"), its views (the activity bar's robot icon, the sessions view, the "Agents: Log in" panel tab), the `agents.session` editor kind, its status item and Output channel, and the dev commands `dev.agents.replay` and `dev.agents.send`.
  - No `extension/` API was removed: everything the extension used is also used by another extension. `api.Editors().OpenProposedChange` stays, available to every extension.
  - Manual check 13.1 (the adapter against the real program) is withdrawn.
- **Moved:** `extensions/internal/pty`, an exported, cgo-free package: `Start(ctx, Options)` and a `Process` with `Read`, `Write`, `Resize`, `Signal`, `Stop` (SIGTERM, then SIGKILL after `Grace`), `Close`, `Done`, `ExitCode` and `Pid`. The program leads a new session with the pseudo-terminal as its controlling terminal, and its whole process group ends with it or with ctx. Tags `PtyOpenFailed`, `ProcessStartFailed`, `PtyWriteFailed`, `PtyReadFailed`, `PtyResizeFailed`, each with a route test.
- **Old state:** `shellState.restoreExtensionTab` (`internal/gui/app/state.go`) drops a saved tab whose extension is not in Emetos or no longer declares its kind (`exthost.Scope.DeclaresEditorKind`, which reads the manifests, so a switched-off extension keeps its tabs). The restore logs one line: `saved tabs of extensions that Emetos no longer has were dropped tabs=[agents/agents.session ...]`. The saved state of the extension's views and its own state files are left untouched (never delete user data).
- **Other changes:** the Relay host's security note no longer says "drive the Agents extension"; comments in five files; the SPEC's §10.15, §9.x and §15.2 lose their Agents mentions (§10.16's removal note, D26 and M13 stay as history); decision 35 is withdrawn.
- **Tests:**
  - `TestTabsOfAnExtensionThatEmetosNoLongerHasAreDropped` (`internal/gui/app/exteditors_test.go`, isolated `XDG_*`): two `agents.session` tabs, an undeclared kind of a real extension, nothing to drop; it asserts the remaining tabs, no error, no notification, one log line, and fails with the drop turned off.
  - `TestTheScopeSaysWhichExtensionDeclaresAnEditorKind` (`internal/gui/exthost/editors_test.go`).
  - `extensions/internal/pty/pty_test.go`: round trips with and without echo, the size and `Resize`, the session and group leader, `Stop`, `Close`, `Signal` and ctx ending the whole group, a `Read` woken by `Close`, and the route of every tag.
  - `TestDesktopMarkdownPreview` counts one editor action fewer; the D4 panel-tabs test still overflows at 760 px with the tabs left (Go Run and Go Tests go into the `...` menu).
- **Goldens:** the 12 Relay goldens (`client_events`, `client_send`, `client_watchonly`, `tab_on`, `tab_off`, `tab_port_taken`, dark and light): the shorter security note wraps differently and the text below it moves up. The agent looked at each, old beside new.
- **README screenshots:** 10 regenerated by `scripts/screenshots.sh`, the `agents` one deleted. I looked at them old beside new: the robot icon and the "AGENTS: LOG IN" tab are gone, the editor title has one action fewer, and the Command Palette lists 613 commands instead of 618.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display` and `emetos_gopls`) · golangci-lint ✅ (also `--build-tags emetos_display`) · error-tags --untested ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean (no module removed) · go test -race ✅ (my run, 98 packages, exit 0) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ (my run, 98 packages, exit 0) · `scripts/realinput.sh` ✅ (the agent's second run; the first had `TestDesktopMarkdownPreview`, fixed, and `TestRealInputBigPreviewStartDoesNotWaitForThePreview` 91 ms over its 150 ms budget, which passed 3 of 3 alone and in the full rerun).
- **Desktop pass:** `scripts/realinput.sh --desktop` in a nested sway on the user's desktop (Radeon 8060S, Vulkan, `EMETOS_REALINPUT_RACE=0`): all 101 tests and subtests passed, exit 0. I looked at the 83 screenshots on contact sheets: no Agents view, robot icon, panel tab or menu row is left, and the panel tabs overflow into the `...` menu as before.
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none new.

## Step 17d.1: The emulator

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17d.1-emulator
- **Summary:** Emetos's own terminal emulator, with no UI (`extensions/terminal/emulator`), written in Go from ECMA-48, the DEC manuals and parser state machine of vt100.net, "XTerm Control Sequences" and `infocmp xterm-256color`. Nothing of xterm.js or of any other terminal was used, read or copied.
- **API:** `New(rows, cols, Options)`; the owner goroutine calls `Write`, `Resize`, `SetScrollback`, `Reset` and `SetTheme`; the UI takes `Snapshot(*Frame)` (only the changed rows, under one lock, with the cursor, the modes, the style and link tables and the palette); `ReadLines`, `LineText` and `Text` read the scrollback; callbacks for replies, the title, the bell, the working folder (OSC 7), the palette, the clipboard and the OSC codes it does not handle (for shell integration, 17d.5); `EncodeKey`, `EncodePaste`, `EncodeFocus` and `EncodeMouse`; `Serialize`.
- **Built:** the C0 and C1 controls, the ESC and CSI sequences of cursor movement, erasing, editing, scroll regions, tabs and character sets; SGR with 16, 256 and true colors and every attribute of SPEC §10.25 (underline styles and colors too); the DEC private modes of §10.25 (the alternate screen, bracketed paste, mouse tracking with the 1006 and 1015 encodings, focus events, synchronized output, the cursor); the device status and attribute replies; OSC 0/1/2, 4, 10/11/12, 7, 8 and 52 (to a callback); wide characters, combining marks, reflow on resize and serialization. Each handler cites its document. What is ignored (sixel, rectangular operations, the kitty keyboard protocol and others) is listed in SPEC §10.25 "As built (step 17d.1)". Limits bound every size (2,000×2,000 cells, 32 parameters, a 64 KiB OSC string, a 16,384-cell reflowed line).
- **Speed** (my run, 160×45, no `-race`; SPEC §14): plain ASCII 854 MB/s (budget 400), a color change every 8 characters 241 MB/s (100), CJK and emoji 371 MB/s (150), scrolling in a region 400 MB/s, cursor motion 228 MB/s, a new true color every 8 characters 110 MB/s; 10,000 lines of scrollback at 160 columns 13.4 MB (limit 16 MB); an idle snapshot, a write with a snapshot and a scroll with a snapshot allocate nothing. `TestThroughputBudgets`, `TestScrollbackMemory` and `TestSnapshotAllocations` hold them.
- **Tests:** table-driven from the documents (every sequence with its defaults and limits, the parser's states with input cut at every byte, C1, invalid UTF-8, scroll regions, the alternate screen, wide characters at the edge, combining marks, reflow, the key and mouse tables, the replies); a serialization round trip over seeded random sequences (60,000 seeds passed once, 400 in the suite; it found about ten bugs, all fixed); invariants checked after every random, fuzzed and recorded input; `vim`, `less`, `htop`, `tmux` and `git log --color` recorded through the test's own pseudo-terminal in a temporary `HOME`, with goldens of the cells, and the recordings replayed without the programs; `FuzzParser` (30 s, `-parallel=1`, no failure); the archtest rule `EmulatorImportsUI`.
- **Decisions:** 120 to 126 of `docs/DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW.md`. Decision 121 (ED 2 does not push the screen into the scrollback) differs from what VS Code shows after Ctrl+L, and step 17d.2 changes it.
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display`) · golangci-lint ✅ (also `--build-tags emetos_display`) · error-tags --untested ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · go test -race ✅ (my run, 99 packages, exit 0) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ (my run, 99 packages, exit 0).
- **Proof:** none (the emulator has no UI; the goldens of the recorded programs are its proof).
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** none new.

## Step 17d.2: The Terminal panel

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17d.2-terminal-panel
- **Summary:** terminals in the panel, as VS Code has them. Ctrl+\` opens a shell in a pseudo-terminal, drawn by Emetos's own emulator (17d.1) with a glyph atlas, with several terminals, splits, the tabs list, selection, copy and paste, the scrollback, the context menu and VS Code's commands, keys, context keys, settings and colors. Nothing of xterm.js or of another terminal was used, read or copied; `foot` was only run, as a program, for the comparison.
- **The emulator's ED 2:** on the normal screen, `CSI 2 J` now moves the lines up to the last non-blank one into the scrollback before it clears, as VS Code shows after Ctrl+L in bash (decision 121 updated). ED 3 still clears the scrollback.
- **Structure** (`extensions/terminal`):
  - `Terminal` (`session.go`) is the model and does not know where it is shown. It holds the process, the emulator, and reader, parser and writer goroutines, with flow control (64 KiB reads, a pause above 100,000 bytes, a resume below 5,000).
  - The pane (`pane.go`, `pane_input.go`) handles keys, the mouse, selection, the wheel, the scrollbar, the input method and bracketed paste with VS Code's multi-line warning (a bar, not a dialog).
  - The widget (`atlas.go`, `grid.go`, `render.go`, `shapes.go`, `decor.go`) draws the cells with two `DrawAtlas` calls. Box-drawing, block, braille and Powerline characters are drawn as shapes. A frame with no change paints nothing.
  - `terminals.go`, `panel.go` and `terminal.go` hold the groups, the tabs list, the splits, the commands and the manifest.
- **New in the extension API:**
  - `Contributes.ContextKeys`;
  - `ViewContent.AllowCommand` (VS Code's `commandsToSkipShell`);
  - `ViewHandle.Hide`;
  - `extension.MenuTerminal`;
  - the refusal class `terminal` for Relay clients;
  - `ui.SplitView` and `ui.ContextMenu`.
  - Also: `pty.ForegroundPid`, and the dev query `dev.terminals`.
- **Registered** with VS Code's ids and Linux keys:
  - toggle, new, split, kill, focus, next and previous, pane focus and resize, copy, paste, select all, clear and the scroll commands;
  - the 15 context keys, the `terminal.integrated.*` settings and every `terminal.*` color.
  - Left for 17d.3: find, links, `runSelectedText`, `runActiveFile`, `sendSequence`, `rename` and `selectDefaultShell`.
  - Declared but not acted on yet: `confirmOnKill`, `confirmOnExit`, `smoothScrolling`, `enableBell` (decisions 127 to 136).
- **Speed** (the agent's runs: release build, headless sway, llvmpipe; `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` "Terminal panel (step 17d.2)"):

  | Target | Budget | Emetos | foot |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | `cat` of 100 MB | under 1.5 s | 623 to 661 ms (231×52) | 466 to 660 ms (160×45), 479 to 575 ms (231×52) |
  | Frames during the `cat` | none over 16 ms | none | |
  | A full screen that all changed (UI thread) | 3 ms or less | 1.55 to 1.97 ms worst (0.52 to 0.56 average) | |
  | Typing (UI thread) | under 2 ms | 1.1 to 1.7 ms worst | |

  **The manager's GPU run** (`scripts/terminal-perf.sh --desktop`, nested sway on the user's desktop, Radeon 8060S, Vulkan): the `cat` of 100 MB took 624 ms by the shell's clock (foot: 768 ms at 160×45 and 681 ms at 231×52), with the end on the screen 968 ms after the command, and no frame over 16 ms (the renderer 1.22 ms on average and 2.41 ms at worst); 6,000 full screens of 160×45 in 434 ms, the UI thread 2.18 ms and the renderer 3.53 ms at worst (budgets 3 and 4 ms); typing, the UI thread 1.12 ms and the renderer 1.97 ms at worst. Every terminal budget of SPEC §14 is met on the GPU.
- **Tests:**
  - **The model:** with a test shell, `/bin/sh` and a fake process.
  - **The extension over the fake API:** commands, split, focus, kill, exit, a failed start, `AllowCommand`, the settings.
  - **The pane:** keys, mouse, selection, paste, wheel, scrollbar, the alternate screen, idle frames.
  - **Settings and profiles.**
  - **Benchmarks of a full frame and of an echo.**
  - **Route tests** of the new tags.
  - **Goldens**, dark and light: a prompt with colors and shapes, a selection, the alternate screen, the cursor styles, and the tabs list with a split. They showed that default-colored text was nearly invisible in the light theme; the emulator now gets the theme's colors.
  - **Real input** (`cmd/emetos/terminal_display_test.go`; every shell is `bash --norc --noprofile` in an isolated `HOME`): Ctrl+\` and `echo hello`; Ctrl+Shift+C and V; wheel scrollback; Ctrl+L keeps the scrollback; a second terminal and a split; Ctrl+P from a terminal; `exit`.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/17d.2-terminal.png` (a colored `ls`, a split and the tabs list). I looked at it. Also `docs/screenshots/terminal.png` for the README.
- **Other:** `scripts/realinput.sh` defaults to `-timeout 40m`; `scripts/terminal-perf.sh` measures the terminal's budgets (`--desktop` for the GPU).
- **Checks:** gofmt ✅ · go vet ✅ (also `emetos_display` and `emetos_gopls`) · golangci-lint ✅ (also `--build-tags emetos_display`) · error-tags --untested ✅ · `go mod tidy -diff` clean · go test -race ✅ (my run after the rebase, 100 packages, exit 0) · CGO_ENABLED=0 build ✅ · CGO_ENABLED=0 test ✅ (my run, 100 packages, exit 0) · `scripts/realinput.sh` (my run, whole suite): one failure, `TestRealInputBigPreviewStartDoesNotWaitForThePreview` (+410 ms over its 150 ms margin). Run alone 3 times it gave −190, +471 and −44 ms: the test compares two cold starts of 4.5 s whose noise is ±500 ms, and it failed the same way before this step (17c.1). Every terminal test passed. The test is being fixed on its own branch (`fix/preview-start-test-noise`).
- **Manual checks:** none
- **Glutter issues:** two new entries:
  - There is no way to rasterize one glyph into an image, so the atlas draws paragraphs and reads them back with the software rasterizer.
  - A focused pane that leaves the tree is not told that it lost the focus. `paneView.unmounted` works around it.

## Step 17e.1: Remove Notes, TODOs and Scratchpad

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17e.1-remove-notes-todo-scratchpad
- **Summary:** the Notes, TODOs and Scratchpad extensions are gone, at the user's request of 2026-10-28 (SPEC §10.26, D28). The first start after the removal writes what a user wrote in a note or in a Scratchpad tab to Markdown files in `$XDG_DATA_HOME/emetos/saved-notes/`, once (a marker file), reading the formats the removed extensions saved, and tells the user with a notification whose **Open Folder** button opens the folder in a new workspace tab. The tests that used the extensions as examples of the contract moved to a second test extension.

  - `extensions/notes`, `extensions/todo`, `extensions/scratchpad` with their tests and 10 goldens, their lines in `extensions/all.go`, `testdata/proof/11.7`, `docs/screenshots/todos-tasks.png`, and the display tests `TestDesktopNotes`, `TestDesktopTodos`, `TestDesktopEmptyNotesViewHasAWelcomeButton`, `TestDesktopFastKeysNotes`, `TestRealInputNotesTitleAndBody`, `TestRealInputScratchpad` and the "note" proof of 17b.1.
  - Gone with them: the TODOs view and `emetos.todo.tags`, the Notes container, `emetos.notes.newNote`, `emetos.notes.deleteNote` and its editor title action, the Scratchpad "+" entry. No `extension/` API was added or removed (only comments that used their ids as examples).

## Step 17e.3: Find All References and Find All Implementations

- **Date:** 2026-10-28
- **Branch:** feat/step-17e.3-references (rebased onto 17e.1)
- **Summary:** a right click on a method or a variable offers **Find All References** and **Find All Implementations** (the editor context menu, group `0_navigation`, when the language has the provider), and the results fill a **References** view in the side bar, as VS Code's built-in references-view does (SPEC §10.28, D30). Shift+Alt+F12 runs Find All References, F4 and Shift+F4 walk the results.
- **The extension** (`extensions/references`, `emetos.references`): the commands with VS Code's ids (`references-view.findReferences`, `findImplementations`, `next`, `prev`, `refresh`, `clear`, `clearHistory`, `showHistory`, `removeReferenceItem`, `copy`, `copyAll`, `copyPath`, and the legacy `references-view.find`); the view: a tree by file with a count, each location's line cut as VS Code's `getPreviewChunks` with the symbol highlighted, the message ("N results in M files"), the nearest result selected, a click opens a preview; the history, run again at the place the word is now (VS Code's `WordAnchor`); the title actions and the item menu. Ported code says so.
- **New in `extension/`:** `Languages().References` and `Implementations` (asynchronous, `done` on the UI thread; they ask the same registry as `editor.action.goToReferences`), `Documents().Active()`, `Clipboard().WriteText`, `ViewHandle.SetTitle`, and the context menu in `extension/ui`.
- **Not built:** Show Call Hierarchy and Show Type Hierarchy, the highlights of the matches in the editors, dragging a result, `references.preferredLocation`, the file icons of the rows (decision 165).
- **Tests:** the model, the preview chunks, the history and its anchors, every command, the view, goldens (results, history and empty, dark and light), the host (`exthost/references_test.go`, `app/extreferences_test.go`), the context menu in a real workspace (the entries only with the provider, in VS Code's order), every new tag's route; real input with a fake language server (`TestDesktopFindAllReferences`: the menu, both titles, Shift+Alt+F12, clicks, F4 and Shift+F4, Dismiss, Clear and the history) and with the real `gopls` (`emetos_gopls`: a method gives "4 results in 3 files", the implementations of an interface method "2 results in 1 file", Shift+Alt+F12 on a variable "3 results in 1 file").
- **Open item (closed by "Fix: four tests that failed when the machine was loaded"):** the fake-server test clicked a file row up to three times, because one click seemed to fail to expand it; the row was open already and the click closed it.
- **Proof:** `docs/implemented-proof-images/17e.3-references.png` (the context menu with the two entries over the References view). I looked at it.
- **Decisions:** 160 to 169 of `docs/DECISIONS-TO-REVIEW.md`.
